Stage 1 — 데이터 모델 + Config CRUD 【1주】

전제: v0202는 그대로 유지, v0203을 병렬 신규 작성 (마이그레이션 없음)
목표: UI·API 없이 C++ 유닛 레벨에서 Step/Profile/Schedule CRUD 완전 동작
검증: Serial/CLI + 유닛 테스트 (Stage 7에서 종합 검증)

---

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arduino.h>

namespace A20_Const {

// ======================================================
// [v0203] 버전
// ======================================================
constexpr char FW_VERSION[]        = "FW_Ver_2.0.3";
constexpr char FW_VERSION_SEMVER[] = "2.0.3";
constexpr char CFG_JSON_FILE[]     = "/json/10_cfg_jsonFile.json";
constexpr char G_JSON_VERSION[]    = "0203";

// ======================================================
// [v0203] Step / Profile / Schedule 개수
// ======================================================
constexpr uint8_t MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE = 8;   // v0202 MAX_SEGMENTS_PER_PROFILE 승계
constexpr uint8_t MAX_PROFILES          = 6;   // v0202 MAX_USER_PROFILES 승계
constexpr uint8_t MAX_SCHEDULES         = 8;   // v0202 동일

// ======================================================
// [v0203] Step durationMin 범위
// ======================================================
constexpr uint16_t MIN_STEP_DURATION_MIN = 1;      // 1분
constexpr uint16_t MAX_STEP_DURATION_MIN = 1440;   // 24시간

// ======================================================
// [v0203] Session / Runtime 상수
// ======================================================
constexpr uint32_t G_A00_WDT_TIMEOUT_SEC        = 10;
constexpr uint32_t G_A00_LOOP_DELAY_MS          = 10;

constexpr int      G_TIME_YEAR_MAX       = 2099;

// 문자열
```

3. A20_Const_Sch_0203.h — 핵심 데이터 모델 ★

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arduino.h>
#include "A20_Const_Const_0203.h"

/* ======================================================
 * [v0203] 3계층 데이터 모델
 *  Step     : 원자 블록
 *  Profile  : Step 시퀀스 (재생 가능한 플레이리스트)
 *  Schedule : 시간 트리거 + profileId 참조만
 * ====================================================== */

/* ======================================================
 * Step Type
 * ====================================================== */
typedef enum : uint8_t {
    EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET = 0,   // 프리셋+스타일 재생
    EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_FIXED  = 1,   // PWM 고정
    EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_REST   = 2,   // 팬 정지 (휴지)
    EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_COUNT
} EN_A20_step_type_t;

typedef struct {
    const EN_A20_step_type_t idx;
    const char*              code;
    const char*              name;
} ST_A20_StepType_t;

inline constexpr ST_A20_StepType_t G_A20_StepType_Arr[EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_COUNT] = {
    { EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET, "PRESET", "Preset" },
    { EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_FIXED,  "FIXED",  "Fixed"  },
    { EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_REST,   "REST",   "Rest"   },
};

/* ======================================================
 * Step Override (세그먼트 개별 물리 파라미터 덮어쓰기)
 * - v0202 ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t 재사용 (하위 호환)
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    bool  enabled;
    float windIntensity;    // -1.0 = 프리셋 값 유지
    float windVariability;  // -1.0 = 프리셋 값 유지
    float gustFrequency;    // -1.0 = 프리셋 값 유지
} ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t;

// v0203 별칭 (명확성)
typedef ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t ST_A20_StepOverride_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Step (원자 블록)
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    uint8_t            stepNo;         // 1..N (Profile 내 순서)
    uint16_t           durationMin;    // 지속 시간 (1~1440)
    EN_A20_step_type_t type;

    // type=PRESET
    char presetCode[A20_Const::MAX_CODE_LEN];
    char styleCode[A20_Const::MAX_CODE_LEN];

    // type=FIXED
    float fixedSpeed;                  // 0~100%

    // 공통 (선택)
    ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t override;
} ST_A20_Step_t;

/* ======================================================
 * AutoOff / Motion (v0202와 동일)
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    struct { bool enabled; uint32_t minutes; } timer;
    struct { bool enabled; char time[6]; }     offTime;
    struct { bool enabled; float temp; }       offTemp;
} ST_A20_AutoOff_t;

typedef struct {
    struct { bool enabled; uint16_t holdSec; } pir;
} ST_A20_Motion_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Profile (재생 가능한 플레이리스트)
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    uint8_t  profileId;                          // 자동 발급 1~255
    uint16_t profileNo;                          // 사용자 지정 1~65535, 유니크
    char     name[A20_Const::MAX_NAME_LEN];
    bool     enabled;

    bool     repeat;
    uint8_t  repeatCount;                        // 0=무한

    uint8_t       stepCount;                     // 1~8
    ST_A20_Step_t steps[A20_Const::MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE];

    ST_A20_AutoOff_t autoOff;
    ST_A20_Motion_t  motion;
} ST_A20_ProfileItem_t;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t              count;
    ST_A20_ProfileItem_t items[A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES];
} ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t;

// [하위 호환]
typedef ST_A20_ProfileItem_t  ST_A20_UserProfileItem_t;
typedef ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t ST_A20_UserProfilesRoot_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Schedule (참조만)
 * - segments[] 완전 삭제
 * - profileId 참조만 유지
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    bool    enabled;
    uint8_t days[7];
    char    startTime[6];
    char    endTime[6];
} ST_A20_SchedulePeriod_t;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t  schId;
    uint16_t schNo;
    char     name[A20_Const::MAX_NAME_LEN];
    bool     enabled;

    ST_A20_SchedulePeriod_t period;
    uint8_t                 profileId;    // ★ 참조 (0=무효)
} ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t               count;
    ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t items[A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES];
} ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Compile-time Consistency
 * ====================================================== */
static_assert((sizeof(G_A20_StepType_Arr) / sizeof(G_A20_StepType_Arr[0])) 
              == EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_COUNT,
              "G_A20_StepType_Arr size mismatch");
```

5. A23_Com_ResetCfg_0203.h — Reset Defaults

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "A20_Const_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"   // v0202 이관
#include "A22_Com_Lookup_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// Step
// ======================================================
static inline void A20_resetStepDefault(ST_A20_Step_t& s) {
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.stepNo      = 1;
    s.durationMin = 20;
    s.type        = EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET;
    A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(s.presetCode,
        A20_Const::DEFAULT_PRESET_CODE, sizeof(s.presetCode));
    A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(s.styleCode,
        A20_Const::DEFAULT_STYLE_CODE,  sizeof(s.styleCode));
    s.override.enabled         = false;
    s.override.windIntensity   = -1.0f;
    s.override.windVariability = -1.0f;
    s.override.gustFrequency   = -1.0f;
}

// ======================================================
// AutoOff / Motion (v0202 승계)
// ======================================================
static inline void A20_resetAutoOffDefault(ST_A20_AutoOff_t& p) {
    p.timer.enabled   = false;  p.timer.minutes = 0;
    p.offTime.enabled = false;  memset(p.offTime.time, 0, sizeof(p.offTime.time));
    p.offTemp.enabled = false;  p.offTemp.temp   = 0.0f;
}

static inline void A20_resetMotionDefault(ST_A20_Motion_t& m) {
    m.pir.enabled = false;
    m.pir.holdSec = 0;
}

// ======================================================
// Profile
// ======================================================
static inline void A20_resetProfileItemDefault(ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& p) {
    memset(&p, 0, sizeof(p));
    p.profileId   = 0;
    p.profileNo   = 0;
    A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(p.name, "", sizeof(p.name));
    p.enabled     = true;
    p.repeat      = false;
    p.repeatCount = 0;
    p.stepCount   = 0;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < A20_Const::MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE; i++) {
        A20_resetStepDefault(p.steps[i]);
    }
    A20_resetAutoOffDefault(p.autoOff);
    A20_resetMotionDefault(p.motion);
}

inline void A20_resetProfilesDefault(ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t& root) {
    memset(&root, 0, sizeof(root));
    root.count = 0;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES; i++) {
        A20_resetProfileItemDefault(root.items[i]);
    }
}

// ======================================================
// Schedule
// ======================================================
static inline void A20_resetSchedulePeriodDefault(ST_A20_SchedulePeriod_t& period) {
    for (uint8_t d = 0; d < 7; d++) period.days[d] = 1;
    A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(period.startTime, "08:00", sizeof(period.startTime));
    A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(period.endTime,   "20:00", sizeof(period.endTime));
    period.enabled = true;
}

static inline void A20_resetScheduleItemDefault(ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t& s) {
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.schId     = 0;
    s.schNo     = 0;
    A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(s.name, "", sizeof(s.name));
    s.enabled   = true;
    A20_resetSchedulePeriodDefault(s.period);
    s.profileId = 0;    // 참조 없음
}

inline void A20_resetSchedulesDefault(ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& root) {
    memset(&root, 0, sizeof(root));
    root.count = 0;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES; i++) {
        A20_resetScheduleItemDefault(root.items[i]);
    }
}

// ======================================================
// Config Root 전체
// ======================================================
inline void A20_resetToDefault(ST_A20_ConfigRoot_t& root) {
    if (root.system)       A20_resetSystemDefault(*root.system);
    if (root.wifi)         A20_resetWifiDefault(*root.wifi);
    if (root.motion)       A20_resetMotionDefault(*root.motion);   // 상위 motion config는 v0202
    if (root.nvsSpec)      A20_resetNvsSpecDefault(*root.nvsSpec);
    if (root.windDict)     A20_resetWindDictDefault(*root.windDict);
    if (root.schedules)    A20_resetSchedulesDefault(*root.schedules);
    if (root.profiles)     A20_resetProfilesDefault(*root.profiles);
    if (root.webPage)      A20_resetWebPageDefault(*root.webPage);
}
```

6. A22_Com_Lookup_0203.h — Step Type 매핑

```cpp
#pragma once
#include "A20_Const_Sch_0203.h"

inline EN_A20_step_type_t A20_stepTypeFromString(const char* p_str) {
    if (!p_str || !p_str[0]) return EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_COUNT; i++) {
        if (strcasecmp(p_str, G_A20_StepType_Arr[i].code) == 0) {
            return G_A20_StepType_Arr[i].idx;
        }
    }
    return EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET;
}

inline const char* A20_stepTypeToString(EN_A20_step_type_t type) {
    if (type >= EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_COUNT) {
        return G_A20_StepType_Arr[EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET].code;
    }
    return G_A20_StepType_Arr[type].code;
}
```

```cpp
// ======================================================

  public:
    // =================================================

    static bool patchProfilesFromJson(ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t& cfg, const JsonDocument& patch);

    static bool saveSchedules(const ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& cfg);

    static bool updateScheduleFromJson(uint16_t schId, const JsonDocument& patch);
    static bool deleteSchedule(uint16_t schId);
    static void toJson_Schedules(const ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& cfg, JsonDocument& doc);

    // =================================================
    // [v0203 신규] 참조 무결성
    // =================================================
    // Profile 삭제 시 호출: 해당 profileId 참조 Schedule → enabled=false
    static uint8_t invalidateSchedulesByProfile(uint8_t profileId);

    // =================================================
    // [v0202 승계] 나머지 시스템/네트워크/WS 등
    // =================================================
    // ... (v0202 C10_Config_0203.h와 동일)

  private:
    static SemaphoreHandle_t     s_recursiveMutex;
    static portMUX_TYPE          s_rootSwapMux;
    static portMUX_TYPE          s_dirtyflagSpinlock;

    static bool _dirty_profiles;
    static bool _dirty_schedules;
    // ... 나머지 dirty (system/wifi/motion/...)

    static ST_A20_cfg_jsonFile_t s_cfgJsonFileMap;

    static bool _loadCfgJsonFile();
};
```

8. C10_Config_Profile_0203.cpp — Profile CRUD

```cpp
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"
#include "A23_Com_ResetCfg_0203.h"
#include "A22_Com_Lookup_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// 내부 헬퍼
// ======================================================
static bool C10_validateStep(const ST_A20_Step_t& s, const char* caller) {
    // durationMin 범위
    if (s.durationMin < A20_Const::MIN_STEP_DURATION_MIN ||
        s.durationMin > A20_Const::MAX_STEP_DURATION_MIN) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
            "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u: durationMin=%u out of [%u,%u]",
            caller, s.stepNo, s.durationMin,
            A20_Const::MIN_STEP_DURATION_MIN,
            A20_Const::MAX_STEP_DURATION_MIN);
        return false;
    }

    switch (s.type) {
        case EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET:
            if (s.presetCode[0] == '\0' || s.styleCode[0] == '\0') {
                CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                    "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u: PRESET requires presetCode/styleCode",
                    caller, s.stepNo);
                return false;
            }
            break;

        case EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_FIXED:
            if (s.fixedSpeed < 0.0f || s.fixedSpeed > 100.0f) {
                CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                    "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u: fixedSpeed=%.1f out of [0,100]",
                    caller, s.stepNo, s.fixedSpeed);
                return false;
            }
            break;

        case EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_REST:
            // 휴지는 추가 필드 없음
            break;

        default:
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u: unknown step type=%u",
                caller, s.stepNo, (unsigned)s.type);
            return false;
    }
    return true;
}

static bool C10_validateProfile(const ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& p, const char* caller) {
    // profileNo 유니크 (1~65535)
    if (p.profileNo == 0) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
            "[C10][%s] profileNo=0 invalid", caller);
        return false;
    }

    // stepCount
    if (p.stepCount == 0 || p.stepCount > A20_Const::MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
            "[C10][%s] profileNo=%u: stepCount=%u out of [1,%u]",
            caller, p.profileNo, p.stepCount, A20_Const::MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE);
        return false;
    }

    // steps 검증
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < p.stepCount; i++) {
        if (!C10_validateStep(p.steps[i], caller)) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// ======================================================
// JSON 디코드 (step)
// ======================================================
static bool C10_fromJson_Step(const JsonObjectConst& j, ST_A20_Step_t& s, const char* caller) {
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.stepNo      = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint8_t>(j, "stepNo", 1);
    s.durationMin = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(j, "durationMin", 20);

    const char* t = A40_ComFunc::Json_getStr(j, "type", "PRESET");
    s.type = A20_stepTypeFromString(t);

    A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(j, "presetCode", s.presetCode, sizeof(s.presetCode), "", caller);
    A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(j, "styleCode",  s.styleCode,  sizeof(s.styleCode),  "", caller);

    s.fixedSpeed = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<float>(j, "fixedSpeed", 0.0f);

    // override
    s.override.enabled         = false;
    s.override.windIntensity   = -1.0f;
    s.override.windVariability = -1.0f;
    s.override.gustFrequency   = -1.0f;

    JsonObjectConst ovr = j["override"].as<JsonObjectConst>();
    if (!ovr.isNull()) {
        s.override.enabled = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(ovr, "enabled", false);
        if (ovr["windIntensity"].is<float>())
            s.override.windIntensity = ovr["windIntensity"].as<float>();
        if (ovr["windVariability"].is<float>())
            s.override.windVariability = ovr["windVariability"].as<float>();
        if (ovr["gustFrequency"].is<float>())
            s.override.gustFrequency = ovr["gustFrequency"].as<float>();
    }
    return true;
}

static void C10_toJson_Step(JsonObject& j, const ST_A20_Step_t& s) {
    j["stepNo"]      = s.stepNo;
    j["durationMin"] = s.durationMin;
    j["type"]        = A20_stepTypeToString(s.type);

    if (s.type == EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET) {
        j["presetCode"] = s.presetCode;
        j["styleCode"]  = s.styleCode;
    } else if (s.type == EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_FIXED) {
        j["fixedSpeed"] = s.fixedSpeed;
    }

    if (s.override.enabled) {
        JsonObject ovr = j["override"].to<JsonObject>();
        ovr["enabled"]         = true;
        ovr["windIntensity"]   = s.override.windIntensity;
        ovr["windVariability"] = s.override.windVariability;
        ovr["gustFrequency"]   = s.override.gustFrequency;
    }
}

// ======================================================
// JSON 디코드/인코드 (profile)
// ======================================================
static bool C10_fromJson_Profile(const JsonObjectConst& jp, ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& p, const char* caller) {
    memset(&p, 0, sizeof(p));

    p.profileId = (uint8_t)A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(jp, "profileId", 0);
    p.profileNo = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(jp, "profileNo", 0);
    A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(jp, "name", p.name, sizeof(p.name), "", caller);
    p.enabled     = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(jp, "enabled", true);
    p.repeat      = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(jp, "repeat", false);
    p.repeatCount = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint8_t>(jp, "repeatCount", 0);

    // steps[]
    p.stepCount = 0;
    JsonArrayConst arr = A40_ComFunc::Json_getArr(jp, "steps");
    if (!arr.isNull()) {
        for (JsonObjectConst js : arr) {
            if (p.stepCount >= A20_Const::MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE) break;
            if (!C10_fromJson_Step(js, p.steps[p.stepCount], caller)) return false;
            p.steps[p.stepCount].stepNo = p.stepCount + 1;  // 자동 재부여
            p.stepCount++;
        }
    }

    // autoOff
    JsonObjectConst ao = jp["autoOff"].as<JsonObjectConst>();
    if (!ao.isNull()) {
        p.autoOff.timer.enabled   = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(ao["timer"], "enabled", false);
        p.autoOff.timer.minutes   = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint32_t>(ao["timer"], "minutes", 0);
        p.autoOff.offTime.enabled = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(ao["offTime"], "enabled", false);
        A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(ao["offTime"], "time", p.autoOff.offTime.time, sizeof(p.autoOff.offTime.time), "00:00", caller);
        p.autoOff.offTemp.enabled = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(ao["offTemp"], "enabled", false);
        p.autoOff.offTemp.temp    = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<float>(ao["offTemp"], "temp", 0.0f);
    }

    // motion
    JsonObjectConst mo = jp["motion"].as<JsonObjectConst>();
    if (!mo.isNull()) {
        p.motion.pir.enabled = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(mo["pir"], "enabled", false);
        p.motion.pir.holdSec = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(mo["pir"], "holdSec", 0);
    }

    return true;
}

static void C10_toJson_Profile(JsonObject& jp, const ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& p) {
    jp["profileId"]   = p.profileId;
    jp["profileNo"]   = p.profileNo;
    jp["name"]        = p.name;
    jp["enabled"]     = p.enabled;
    jp["repeat"]      = p.repeat;
    jp["repeatCount"] = p.repeatCount;
    jp["stepCount"]   = p.stepCount;

    JsonArray arr = jp["steps"].to<JsonArray>();
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < p.stepCount && i < A20_Const::MAX_STEPS_PER_PROFILE; i++) {
        JsonObject js = arr.add<JsonObject>();
        C10_toJson_Step(js, p.steps[i]);
    }

    JsonObject ao = jp["autoOff"].to<JsonObject>();
    ao["timer"]["enabled"]   = p.autoOff.timer.enabled;
    ao["timer"]["minutes"]   = p.autoOff.timer.minutes;
    ao["offTime"]["enabled"] = p.autoOff.offTime.enabled;
    ao["offTime"]["time"]    = p.autoOff.offTime.time;
    ao["offTemp"]["enabled"] = p.autoOff.offTemp.enabled;
    ao["offTemp"]["temp"]    = p.autoOff.offTemp.temp;

    JsonObject mo = jp["motion"].to<JsonObject>();
    mo["pir"]["enabled"] = p.motion.pir.enabled;
    mo["pir"]["holdSec"] = p.motion.pir.holdSec;
}

// ======================================================
// Public API
// ======================================================
bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::loadProfiles(ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t& cfg) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    A20_resetProfilesDefault(cfg);

    const char* path = s_cfgJsonFileMap.userProfiles;
    if (!path || !path[0]) return false;

    JsonDocument doc;
    if (!A40_IO::Load_File2JsonDoc_V21(path, doc, true, __func__)) return false;

    JsonArrayConst arr = A40_ComFunc::Json_getArr(doc, "profiles");
    if (arr.isNull()) return true;   // 빈 파일 허용

    for (JsonObjectConst jp : arr) {
        if (cfg.count >= A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES) break;
        ST_A20_ProfileItem_t p;
        if (!C10_fromJson_Profile(jp, p, __func__)) {
            A20_resetProfilesDefault(cfg);
            return false;
        }
        if (!C10_validateProfile(p, __func__)) {
            A20_resetProfilesDefault(cfg);
            return false;
        }
        cfg.items[cfg.count++] = p;
    }
    return true;
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveProfiles(const ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t& cfg) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    const char* path = s_cfgJsonFileMap.userProfiles;
    if (!path || !path[0]) return false;

    JsonDocument doc;
    doc["version"] = A20_Const::G_JSON_VERSION;
    JsonArray arr = doc["profiles"].to<JsonArray>();
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cfg.count && i < A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES; i++) {
        if (!C10_validateProfile(cfg.items[i], __func__)) return false;
        JsonObject jp = arr.add<JsonObject>();
        C10_toJson_Profile(jp, cfg.items[i]);
    }

    return A40_IO::Save_JsonDoc2File_V21(path, doc, true, true, __func__);
}

int CL_C10_ConfigManager::addProfileFromJson(const JsonDocument& doc) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return -1;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.profiles) {
        g_A20_config_root.profiles = new (std::nothrow) ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t();
        if (!g_A20_config_root.profiles) return -1;
        A20_resetProfilesDefault(*g_A20_config_root.profiles);
    }
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.profiles;
    if (root.count >= A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES) return -2;

    JsonObjectConst jp = doc["profile"].is<JsonObjectConst>()
        ? doc["profile"].as<JsonObjectConst>()
        : doc.as<JsonObjectConst>();

    ST_A20_ProfileItem_t p;
    if (!C10_fromJson_Profile(jp, p, __func__)) return -3;

    // 유니크 profileNo
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].profileNo == p.profileNo) {
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                "[C10] addProfile: duplicated profileNo=%u", p.profileNo);
            return -4;
        }
    }

    // 유니크 profileId (자동 발급)
    uint8_t newId = 1;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].profileId >= newId) newId = root.items[i].profileId + 1;
    }
    if (newId == 0) newId = 1;
    p.profileId = newId;

    if (!C10_validateProfile(p, __func__)) return -5;

    int idx = (int)root.count;
    root.items[root.count++] = p;

    _dirty_profiles = true;
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[C10] Profile added: index=%d, profileId=%u, profileNo=%u",
        idx, p.profileId, p.profileNo);
    return idx;
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::updateProfileFromJson(uint16_t profileId, const JsonDocument& patch) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.profiles) return false;
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.profiles;

    int idx = -1;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].profileId == (uint8_t)profileId) { idx = i; break; }
    }
    if (idx < 0) return false;

    JsonObjectConst jp = patch["profile"].is<JsonObjectConst>()
        ? patch["profile"].as<JsonObjectConst>()
        : patch.as<JsonObjectConst>();

    ST_A20_ProfileItem_t p;
    if (!C10_fromJson_Profile(jp, p, __func__)) return false;

    // profileNo 유니크 (자신 제외)
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (i == idx) continue;
        if (root.items[i].profileNo == p.profileNo) return false;
    }
    p.profileId = (uint8_t)profileId;   // 유지

    if (!C10_validateProfile(p, __func__)) return false;

    root.items[idx] = p;
    _dirty_profiles = true;
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[C10] Profile updated: profileId=%u, profileNo=%u",
        profileId, p.profileNo);
    return true;
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::deleteProfile(uint16_t profileId) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.profiles) return false;
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.profiles;

    int idx = -1;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].profileId == (uint8_t)profileId) { idx = i; break; }
    }
    if (idx < 0) return false;

    // 참조 무결성: Schedule 무효화
    uint8_t affected = invalidateSchedulesByProfile((uint8_t)profileId);

    // shift
    for (uint8_t i = (uint8_t)idx + 1; i < root.count; i++) {
        root.items[i - 1] = root.items[i];
    }
    root.count--;

    _dirty_profiles = true;
    if (affected > 0) _dirty_schedules = true;

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[C10] Profile deleted: profileId=%u (affected schedules=%u)",
        profileId, affected);
    return true;
}

void CL_C10_ConfigManager::toJson_Profiles(const ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t& cfg, JsonDocument& doc) {
    JsonObject top = doc.as<JsonObject>();
    top.remove("profiles");

    doc["version"] = A20_Const::G_JSON_VERSION;
    JsonArray arr = doc["profiles"].to<JsonArray>();
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cfg.count && i < A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES; i++) {
        JsonObject jp = arr.add<JsonObject>();
        C10_toJson_Profile(jp, cfg.items[i]);
    }
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::patchProfilesFromJson(ST_A20_ProfilesRoot_t& cfg, const JsonDocument& patch) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    JsonArrayConst arr = A40_ComFunc::Json_getArr(patch, "profiles");
    if (arr.isNull()) return false;

    A20_resetProfilesDefault(cfg);
    uint8_t newId = 1;

    for (JsonObjectConst jp : arr) {
        if (cfg.count >= A20_Const::MAX_PROFILES) break;
        ST_A20_ProfileItem_t p;
        if (!C10_fromJson_Profile(jp, p, __func__)) return false;
        p.profileId = newId++;
        if (!C10_validateProfile(p, __func__)) return false;
        cfg.items[cfg.count++] = p;
    }
    _dirty_profiles = true;
    return true;
}

uint8_t CL_C10_ConfigManager::invalidateSchedulesByProfile(uint8_t profileId) {
    if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules || profileId == 0) return 0;
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.schedules;
    uint8_t affected = 0;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].profileId == profileId) {
            root.items[i].enabled   = false;
            root.items[i].profileId = 0;   // 참조 해제
            affected++;
        }
    }
    return affected;
}
```

9. C10_Config_Sch_0203.cpp — Schedule CRUD (참조만)

```cpp
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"
#include "A23_Com_ResetCfg_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// JSON 디코드/인코드
// ======================================================
static bool C10_fromJson_Schedule(const JsonObjectConst& js, ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t& s, const char* caller) {
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));

    s.schId   = (uint8_t)A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(js, "schId", 0);
    s.schNo   = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(js, "schNo", 0);
    A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(js, "name", s.name, sizeof(s.name), "", caller);
    s.enabled = A40_ComFunc::Json_getBool(js, "enabled", true);

    // period
    JsonObjectConst jp = js["period"].as<JsonObjectConst>();
    if (!jp.isNull()) {
        JsonArrayConst days = A40_ComFunc::Json_getArr(jp, "days");
        for (uint8_t d = 0; d < 7; d++) {
            s.period.days[d] = (days.isNull() || d >= days.size())
                ? 1 : (days[d].as<bool>() ? 1 : 0);
        }
        A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(jp, "startTime", s.period.startTime, sizeof(s.period.startTime), "08:00", caller);
        A40_ComFunc::Json_copyStr(jp, "endTime",   s.period.endTime,   sizeof(s.period.endTime),   "20:00", caller);
    }

    s.profileId = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint8_t>(js, "profileId", 0);
    return true;
}

static void C10_toJson_Schedule(JsonObject& js, const ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t& s) {
    js["schId"]     = s.schId;
    js["schNo"]     = s.schNo;
    js["name"]      = s.name;
    js["enabled"]   = s.enabled;
    js["profileId"] = s.profileId;

    JsonObject jp = js["period"].to<JsonObject>();
    JsonArray days = jp["days"].to<JsonArray>();
    for (uint8_t d = 0; d < 7; d++) days.add(s.period.days[d] ? 1 : 0);
    jp["startTime"] = s.period.startTime;
    jp["endTime"]   = s.period.endTime;
}

// ======================================================
// 유효성: profileId가 실제 존재하는지 검증
// ======================================================
static bool C10_validateScheduleRef(const ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t& s, const char* caller) {
    if (s.schNo == 0) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR, "[C10][%s] schNo=0 invalid", caller);
        return false;
    }
    // profileId=0은 "참조 없음"으로 허용 (비활성)
    if (s.profileId == 0) return true;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.profiles) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
            "[C10][%s] profileId=%u but profiles root null", caller, s.profileId);
        return false;
    }
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < g_A20_config_root.profiles->count; i++) {
        if (g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[i].profileId == s.profileId) return true;
    }
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
        "[C10][%s] profileId=%u does not exist", caller, s.profileId);
    return false;
}

// ======================================================
// Public API
// ======================================================
bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::loadSchedules(ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& cfg) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    A20_resetSchedulesDefault(cfg);

    const char* path = s_cfgJsonFileMap.schedules;
    if (!path || !path[0]) return false;

    JsonDocument doc;
    if (!A40_IO::Load_File2JsonDoc_V21(path, doc, true, __func__)) return false;

    JsonArrayConst arr = A40_ComFunc::Json_getArr(doc, "schedules");
    if (arr.isNull()) return true;

    for (JsonObjectConst js : arr) {
        if (cfg.count >= A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES) break;
        ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t s;
        if (!C10_fromJson_Schedule(js, s, __func__)) {
            A20_resetSchedulesDefault(cfg);
            return false;
        }
        if (!C10_validateScheduleRef(s, __func__)) {
            // 파일 손상: 참조 무효는 로드 실패가 아닌 자동 비활성
            s.enabled = false;
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_WARN,
                "[C10] Schedule schNo=%u reference invalid → disabled", s.schNo);
        }
        cfg.items[cfg.count++] = s;
    }
    return true;
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveSchedules(const ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& cfg) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    const char* path = s_cfgJsonFileMap.schedules;
    if (!path || !path[0]) return false;

    JsonDocument doc;
    doc["version"] = A20_Const::G_JSON_VERSION;
    JsonArray arr = doc["schedules"].to<JsonArray>();
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cfg.count && i < A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES; i++) {
        if (!C10_validateScheduleRef(cfg.items[i], __func__)) return false;
        JsonObject js = arr.add<JsonObject>();
        C10_toJson_Schedule(js, cfg.items[i]);
    }
    return A40_IO::Save_JsonDoc2File_V21(path, doc, true, true, __func__);
}

int CL_C10_ConfigManager::addScheduleFromJson(const JsonDocument& doc) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return -1;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules) {
        g_A20_config_root.schedules = new (std::nothrow) ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t();
        if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules) return -1;
        A20_resetSchedulesDefault(*g_A20_config_root.schedules);
    }
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.schedules;
    if (root.count >= A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES) return -2;

    JsonObjectConst js = doc["schedule"].is<JsonObjectConst>()
        ? doc["schedule"].as<JsonObjectConst>()
        : doc.as<JsonObjectConst>();

    ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t s;
    if (!C10_fromJson_Schedule(js, s, __func__)) return -3;

    if (!C10_validateScheduleRef(s, __func__)) return -4;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].schNo == s.schNo) return -5;
    }

    uint8_t newId = 1;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].schId >= newId) newId = root.items[i].schId + 1;
    }
    s.schId = newId;

    int idx = (int)root.count;
    root.items[root.count++] = s;
    _dirty_schedules = true;
    return idx;
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::updateScheduleFromJson(uint16_t schId, const JsonDocument& patch) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules) return false;
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.schedules;

    int idx = -1;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].schId == (uint8_t)schId) { idx = i; break; }
    }
    if (idx < 0) return false;

    JsonObjectConst js = patch["schedule"].is<JsonObjectConst>()
        ? patch["schedule"].as<JsonObjectConst>()
        : patch.as<JsonObjectConst>();

    ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t s;
    if (!C10_fromJson_Schedule(js, s, __func__)) return false;
    s.schId = (uint8_t)schId;

    if (!C10_validateScheduleRef(s, __func__)) return false;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (i == idx) continue;
        if (root.items[i].schNo == s.schNo) return false;
    }

    root.items[idx] = s;
    _dirty_schedules = true;
    return true;
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::deleteSchedule(uint16_t schId) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules) return false;
    auto& root = *g_A20_config_root.schedules;

    int idx = -1;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < root.count; i++) {
        if (root.items[i].schId == (uint8_t)schId) { idx = i; break; }
    }
    if (idx < 0) return false;

    for (uint8_t i = (uint8_t)idx + 1; i < root.count; i++) {
        root.items[i - 1] = root.items[i];
    }
    root.count--;
    _dirty_schedules = true;
    return true;
}

void CL_C10_ConfigManager::toJson_Schedules(const ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& cfg, JsonDocument& doc) {
    JsonObject top = doc.as<JsonObject>();
    top.remove("schedules");

    doc["version"] = A20_Const::G_JSON_VERSION;
    JsonArray arr = doc["schedules"].to<JsonArray>();
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cfg.count && i < A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES; i++) {
        JsonObject js = arr.add<JsonObject>();
        C10_toJson_Schedule(js, cfg.items[i]);
    }
}

bool CL_C10_ConfigManager::patchSchedulesFromJson(ST_A20_SchedulesRoot_t& cfg, const JsonDocument& patch) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_recursiveMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    JsonArrayConst arr = A40_ComFunc::Json_getArr(patch, "schedules");
    if (arr.isNull()) return false;

    A20_resetSchedulesDefault(cfg);
    uint8_t newId = 1;

    for (JsonObjectConst js : arr) {
        if (cfg.count >= A20_Const::MAX_SCHEDULES) break;
        ST_A20_ScheduleItem_t s;
        if (!C10_fromJson_Schedule(js, s, __func__)) return false;
        s.schId = newId++;
        if (!C10_validateScheduleRef(s, __func__)) return false;
        cfg.items[cfg.count++] = s;
    }
    _dirty_schedules = true;
    return true;
}
```

---

10. C10_Config_Step_0203.cpp — Step 유효성 (공용)

```cpp
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"
#include "A22_Com_Lookup_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// Step 단독 검증 (Profile/Step 편집기에서 재사용)
// ======================================================
bool C10_validateStepStandalone(const ST_A20_Step_t& s, const char* caller) {
    if (s.stepNo == 0) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR, "[C10][%s] stepNo=0 invalid", caller);
        return false;
    }
    if (s.durationMin < A20_Const::MIN_STEP_DURATION_MIN ||
        s.durationMin > A20_Const::MAX_STEP_DURATION_MIN) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
            "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u durationMin=%u out of [%u,%u]",
            caller, s.stepNo, s.durationMin,
            A20_Const::MIN_STEP_DURATION_MIN,
            A20_Const::MAX_STEP_DURATION_MIN);
        return false;
    }
    if (s.type == EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET) {
        if (!s.presetCode[0] || !s.styleCode[0]) {
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u PRESET requires presetCode/styleCode", caller, s.stepNo);
            return false;
        }
    } else if (s.type == EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_FIXED) {
        if (s.fixedSpeed < 0.0f || s.fixedSpeed > 100.0f) {
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                "[C10][%s] stepNo=%u fixedSpeed=%.1f out of [0,100]",
                caller, s.stepNo, s.fixedSpeed);
            return false;
        }
    } else if (s.type == EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_REST) {
        // OK
    } else {
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
```

12. Stage 1 완료 체크리스트 ✅

컴파일

☐ _0203 신규 파일만으로 컴파일 성공 (경고 0건)
☐ 하위 호환 typedef (ST_A20_UserProfileItem_t 등) 정상

CRUD 유닛 검증

Profile:

☐ addProfileFromJson 정상 (profileId 자동 발급, profileNo 유니크)
☐ updateProfileFromJson 정상 (profileId 유지, profileNo 유니크)
☐ deleteProfile 정상
☐ deleteProfile → 참조 Schedule enabled=false + profileId=0 자동
☐ loadProfiles → saveProfiles → 재로드 동일 데이터
☐ patchProfilesFromJson 전체 교체

Schedule:

☐ addScheduleFromJson — profileId 존재 검증 통과
☐ addScheduleFromJson — 존재하지 않는 profileId → 실패 (-4)
☐ updateScheduleFromJson 정상
☐ deleteSchedule 정상
☐ loadSchedules — 손상된 profileId 참조 → enabled=false 자동

Step:

☐ durationMin 0 → 실패
☐ durationMin 1441 → 실패
☐ durationMin 1/1440 → 성공 (경계값)
☐ type=PRESET + presetCode 없음 → 실패
☐ type=FIXED + fixedSpeed=105 → 실패
☐ type=REST → 항상 성공

참조 무결성

☐ Profile 삭제 시 Schedule 다수 참조 → 모두 무효화
☐ Schedule profileId 검증 실패 시 저장 거부

파일 I/O

☐ LittleFS 저장 후 재부팅 → 동일 데이터 복원
☐ .bak 자동 백업 동작
☐ 원본 손상 → .bak 복원

하위 호환

☐ 기존 ST_A20_UserProfileItem_t 이름 사용하는 v0202 코드 그대로 컴파일

2. CT10_Ctl_0203.h — Session Context + FSM ★핵심

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>
#include <atomic>
#include <freertos/FreeRTOS.h>
#include <freertos/semphr.h>

#include "A20_Const_0203.h"
#include "CT10_Monitor_0203.h"
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"
#include "D10_Logger_0203.h"
#include "M10_MotionLogic_0203.h"
#include "P10_PWM_ctrl_0203.h"
#include "S10_Simul_0203.h"
#include "S20_WindSolver_0203.h"
#include "TM10_TimeMg_0203.h"

/* ======================================================
 * [v0203] FSM 5-state (7 → 5 단순화)
 * - OVERRIDE / PROFILE_RUN / SCHEDULE_RUN → RUNNING 통합
 * - 세부는 runCtx.trigger / runCtx.sessionType으로
 * ====================================================== */
typedef enum : uint8_t {
    EN_CT10_STATE_IDLE            = 0,
    EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING         = 1,   // ★ 모든 실행 통합
    EN_CT10_STATE_MOTION_BLOCKED  = 2,
    EN_CT10_STATE_AUTOOFF_STOPPED = 3,
    EN_CT10_STATE_TIME_INVALID    = 4,
} EN_CT10_state_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Session Type / Trigger / Runtime State
 * ====================================================== */
typedef enum : uint8_t {
    EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE     = 0,
    EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST = 1,   // Profile 재생 (Step 시퀀스)
    EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET   = 2,   // 프리셋 1개 즉시 실행
    EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED    = 3,   // PWM 고정 (일반 선풍기)
} EN_CT10_session_type_t;

typedef enum : uint8_t {
    EN_CT10_TRIGGER_NONE     = 0,
    EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL   = 1,   // 사용자 "▶ 재생" / 1~5단
    EN_CT10_TRIGGER_SCHEDULE = 2,   // 시간 윈도우 진입
} EN_CT10_trigger_t;

typedef enum : uint8_t {
    EN_SESSION_ACTIVE    = 0,
    EN_SESSION_PAUSED    = 1,
    EN_SESSION_COMPLETED = 2,
} EN_SESSION_runtime_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Session Context (Manual + Schedule 통합)
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    EN_CT10_session_type_t  type;
    EN_CT10_trigger_t       trigger;
    EN_SESSION_runtime_t    state;

    union {
        struct {
            uint8_t  profileId;
            uint16_t profileNo;
            uint8_t  currentStepIdx;
            uint8_t  totalSteps;
            uint8_t  loopCount;
        } playlist;
        struct {
            char presetCode[A20_Const::MAX_CODE_LEN];
            char styleCode[A20_Const::MAX_CODE_LEN];
            ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t override;
        } preset;
        struct {
            float percent;
        } fixed;
    } payload;

    // 시간 관리
    uint32_t startedAt;
    uint32_t pausedAtMs;
    uint32_t pausedAccumMs;
    uint32_t currentStepStartMs;
    uint32_t endsAt;             // 0=무한

    // Schedule 참조 (trigger=SCHEDULE일 때만)
    uint8_t  activeSchId;
    uint16_t activeSchNo;
} ST_CT10_SessionContext_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Run Context (관측용, Session 상태 + AutoOff 등)
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    EN_CT10_state_t         state;
    EN_CT10_session_type_t  sessionType;
    EN_CT10_trigger_t       trigger;
    EN_SESSION_runtime_t    sessionState;

    uint32_t lastDecisionMs;
    uint32_t lastStateChangeMs;
    uint32_t stateHoldUntilMs;
    bool     stateAckRequired;

    // AutoOff 진행
    bool     autoOffTimerArmed;
    uint32_t autoOffTimerStartMs;
    uint32_t autoOffTimerMinutes;
    bool     autoOffOffTimeEnabled;
    uint16_t autoOffOffTimeMinutes;
    bool     autoOffOffTempEnabled;
    float    autoOffOffTemp;

    // Pending Schedule (Manual 중일 때만)
    bool     pendingScheduleActive;
    uint8_t  pendingSchId;
    uint16_t pendingSchNo;
    char     pendingSchName[A20_Const::MAX_NAME_LEN];
    char     pendingSchStart[6];
} ST_CT10_RunContext_t;

/* ======================================================
 * decideSession 반환
 * ====================================================== */
typedef struct {
    EN_CT10_state_t         state;
    ST_CT10_SessionContext_t session;   // type=NONE이면 세션 없음
    bool                    wantSimStop;
} ST_CT10_Decision_t;

/* ======================================================
 * Control Manager
 * ====================================================== */
class CL_CT10_ControlManager {
  public:
    static CL_CT10_ControlManager& instance();
    static bool begin();
    static void tick();

    // =================================================
    // Session API (통합)
    // =================================================
    static bool startSession(EN_CT10_session_type_t type,
                             EN_CT10_trigger_t      trigger,
                             const JsonDocument&    payloadDoc);

    static bool startPlaylistManual(uint8_t profileId);
    static bool startPresetManual(const char* presetCode, const char* styleCode);
    static bool startFixedManual(float percent);

    static bool pauseSession();
    static bool resumeSession();
    static bool stopSession();

    // =================================================
    // 상태 조회
    // =================================================
    static bool isSessionActive();
    static bool isSessionPaused();
    static ST_CT10_SessionContext_t getSessionSnapshot();

    // =================================================
    // Reload (v0202 승계)
    // =================================================
    static bool reloadAll();
    static bool requestReload();
    static bool consumePendingReload();
    static bool isReloadPending();
    static bool isLastReloadOk();

    // =================================================
    // AutoOff Ack
    // =================================================
    static void ackEvent() { instance().ackEventState(); }

    // =================================================
    // Export
    // =================================================
    void exportStateJson_v02(JsonDocument& doc);
    void exportSummaryJson(JsonDocument& doc);
    void exportMetricsJson(JsonDocument& doc);
    ST_MON_Snapshot_t getMonitorSnapshot();

    // =================================================
    // Dirty
    // =================================================
    void markDirty(const char* key);
    uint8_t consumeAllDirty();

  public:
    // 런타임 (기존 호환)
    CL_P10_PWM* pwm = nullptr;
    bool        active = false;

  private:
    CL_M10_MotionLogic* motion = nullptr;
    CL_S10_Simulation   sim;

    // =================================================
    // Session (단일 소스)
    // =================================================
    ST_CT10_SessionContext_t _session;

    // Run Context (관측용)
    ST_CT10_RunContext_t _runCtx;

    // AutoOff 래치
    bool     _autoOffLatched = false;
    int16_t  _persistOffTimeLastYday = -1;

    // Pending Reload
    inline static std::atomic<bool> s_pendingReload{false};
    inline static std::atomic<bool> s_lastReloadOk{true};

    // Dirty
    bool _dirtyState   = false;
    bool _dirtyMetrics = false;
    bool _dirtyChart   = false;
    bool _dirtySummary = false;

    // 타이머
    uint32_t _lastTickMs = 0;
    uint32_t _lastMetricsPushMs = 0;

    // 상수
    static constexpr uint32_t S_TICK_MIN_INTERVAL_MS     = 40;
    static constexpr uint32_t S_METRICS_PUSH_INTERVAL_MS = 1500;
    static constexpr uint32_t S_EVENT_HOLD_MS            = 3000;

    // Pause 스마트 정책
    static constexpr uint32_t S_PAUSE_CONTINUE_MS   = 5UL  * 60000UL;   // < 5분: 이어서
    static constexpr uint32_t S_PAUSE_STEP_RESET_MS = 30UL * 60000UL;   // 5~30분: Step 리셋

    // Mutex
    static SemaphoreHandle_t s_stateMutex;

    // =================================================
    // 내부 (Session 실행)
    // =================================================
    void tickLoop();
    void tickSession();
    void tickPlaylist();
    void tickPreset();
    void tickFixed();

    bool tickStepSequence(const ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& prof,
                          uint8_t& currentIdx, uint8_t& loopCount);

    void applyStep(const ST_A20_Step_t& step);
    void applyPreset(const char* presetCode, const char* styleCode,
                     const ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t* ovr);
    void applyFixed(float percent);
    void applyRest();

    // =================================================
    // FSM
    // =================================================
    ST_CT10_Decision_t decideSession();
    void               applyDecision(const ST_CT10_Decision_t& d);

    // =================================================
    // AutoOff / Motion / Schedule
    // =================================================
    void initAutoOffFromProfile(const ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& p);
    bool checkAutoOff();
    void onAutoOffTriggered();
    void ackEventState();

    int  findActiveScheduleIndex(bool allowOverlap);
    int  findProfileById(uint8_t profileId);
    void updatePendingScheduleInfo();

    bool isMotionBlocked(const ST_A20_Motion_t& cfg);

    // =================================================
    // 유틸
    // =================================================
    uint32_t sessionElapsedMs() const;
    void     emitStepChange();

    CL_CT10_ControlManager() = default;
};
```

---

3. CT10_Ctl_Core_0203.cpp — start/stop

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"
#include "N10_NvsManager_0203.h"

extern CL_P10_PWM g_P10_pwm;

SemaphoreHandle_t CL_CT10_ControlManager::s_stateMutex = nullptr;

CL_CT10_ControlManager& CL_CT10_ControlManager::instance() {
    static CL_CT10_ControlManager v_inst;
    return v_inst;
}

bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::begin() {
    instance().pwm = &g_P10_pwm;
    auto& self = instance();
    memset(&self._session, 0, sizeof(self._session));
    memset(&self._runCtx,  0, sizeof(self._runCtx));
    self._session.type  = EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE;
    self._session.state = EN_SESSION_COMPLETED;
    self._runCtx.state  = EN_CT10_STATE_IDLE;
    self._autoOffLatched = false;
    self.sim.begin(&g_P10_pwm);
    self.active = true;
    self.markDirty("state");
    self.markDirty("metrics");
    self.markDirty("summary");
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[CT10] begin()");
    return true;
}

void CL_CT10_ControlManager::tick() {
    instance().tickLoop();
}

// ======================================================
// startSession (통합 진입점)
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::startSession(EN_CT10_session_type_t type,
                                          EN_CT10_trigger_t      trigger,
                                          const JsonDocument&    payloadDoc) {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    // 기존 Session 종료 (자동)
    if (self._session.type != EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[CT10] startSession: replacing existing (type=%u)",
            (unsigned)self._session.type);
        self.sim.stop();
        if (self.pwm) self.pwm->setDutyPercent(0);
    }

    // 새 Session 초기화
    memset(&self._session, 0, sizeof(self._session));
    self._session.type    = type;
    self._session.trigger = trigger;
    self._session.state   = EN_SESSION_ACTIVE;
    self._session.startedAt = millis();
    self._session.currentStepStartMs = millis();

    // =================================================
    // type별 payload 파싱
    // =================================================
    switch (type) {
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST: {
            uint16_t profileId = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(
                payloadDoc.as<JsonObjectConst>(), "profileId", 0);
            if (profileId == 0 || profileId > 255) return false;

            int profIdx = self.findProfileById((uint8_t)profileId);
            if (profIdx < 0) {
                CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
                    "[CT10] startSession: profileId=%u not found", profileId);
                return false;
            }
            const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[profIdx];
            if (!prof.enabled || prof.stepCount == 0) return false;

            self._session.payload.playlist.profileId = (uint8_t)profileId;
            self._session.payload.playlist.profileNo = prof.profileNo;
            self._session.payload.playlist.totalSteps = prof.stepCount;
            self._session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx = 0;
            self._session.payload.playlist.loopCount = 0;

            self.initAutoOffFromProfile(prof);
            break;
        }

        case EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET: {
            JsonObjectConst p = payloadDoc.as<JsonObjectConst>();
            const char* pc = A40_ComFunc::Json_getStr(p, "presetCode", "");
            const char* sc = A40_ComFunc::Json_getStr(p, "styleCode", "");
            if (!pc[0] || !sc[0]) return false;

            A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(
                self._session.payload.preset.presetCode,
                pc, sizeof(self._session.payload.preset.presetCode));
            A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(
                self._session.payload.preset.styleCode,
                sc, sizeof(self._session.payload.preset.styleCode));
            self._session.payload.preset.override.enabled = false;
            self._session.payload.preset.override.windIntensity   = -1.0f;
            self._session.payload.preset.override.windVariability = -1.0f;
            self._session.payload.preset.override.gustFrequency   = -1.0f;
            break;
        }

        case EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED: {
            float pct = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<float>(
                payloadDoc.as<JsonObjectConst>(), "percent", 50.0f);
            if (pct < 0.0f) pct = 0.0f;
            if (pct > 100.0f) pct = 100.0f;
            self._session.payload.fixed.percent = pct;
            break;
        }

        default:
            return false;
    }

    // =================================================
    // AutoOff 래치 해제 (새 Session = 사용자 개입)
    // =================================================
    self._autoOffLatched = false;

    // =================================================
    // NVS 영속화 (PLAYLIST MANUAL만)
    // =================================================
    if (type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST && trigger == EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL) {
        CL_N10_NvsManager::setManualSession(
            self._session.payload.playlist.profileNo, true);
    } else {
        CL_N10_NvsManager::setManualSession(0, false);
    }

    self.markDirty("state");
    self.markDirty("metrics");
    self.markDirty("summary");

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[CT10] startSession: type=%u trigger=%u",
        (unsigned)type, (unsigned)trigger);
    return true;
}

// 편의 진입점
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::startPlaylistManual(uint8_t profileId) {
    JsonDocument d;
    d["profileId"] = profileId;
    return startSession(EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST, EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL, d);
}

bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::startPresetManual(const char* pc, const char* sc) {
    JsonDocument d;
    d["presetCode"] = pc;
    d["styleCode"]  = sc;
    return startSession(EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET, EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL, d);
}

bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::startFixedManual(float pct) {
    JsonDocument d;
    d["percent"] = pct;
    return startSession(EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED, EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL, d);
}

// ======================================================
// stopSession
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::stopSession() {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE) return false;

    self.sim.stop();
    if (self.pwm) self.pwm->setDutyPercent(0);

    memset(&self._session, 0, sizeof(self._session));
    self._session.type  = EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE;
    self._session.state = EN_SESSION_COMPLETED;

    // NVS 해제
    CL_N10_NvsManager::setManualSession(0, false);

    self.markDirty("state");
    self.markDirty("metrics");
    self.markDirty("summary");

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[CT10] stopSession()");
    return true;
}

// ======================================================
// 조회
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::isSessionActive() {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;
    return self._session.type != EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE &&
           self._session.state == EN_SESSION_ACTIVE;
}

bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::isSessionPaused() {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;
    return self._session.state == EN_SESSION_PAUSED;
}

ST_CT10_SessionContext_t CL_CT10_ControlManager::getSessionSnapshot() {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    ST_CT10_SessionContext_t empty = {};
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return empty;
    return self._session;
}
```

4. CT10_Ctl_FSM_0203.cpp — decideSession (5단)

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include "N10_NvsManager_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// FSM 문자열 (API 응답용)
// ======================================================
const char* CT10_stateToString(EN_CT10_state_t s) {
    switch (s) {
        case EN_CT10_STATE_IDLE:            return "IDLE";
        case EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING:         return "RUNNING";
        case EN_CT10_STATE_MOTION_BLOCKED:  return "MOTION_BLOCKED";
        case EN_CT10_STATE_AUTOOFF_STOPPED: return "AUTOOFF_STOPPED";
        case EN_CT10_STATE_TIME_INVALID:    return "TIME_INVALID";
        default:                            return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

const char* CT10_triggerToString(EN_CT10_trigger_t t) {
    switch (t) {
        case EN_CT10_TRIGGER_NONE:     return "NONE";
        case EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL:   return "MANUAL";
        case EN_CT10_TRIGGER_SCHEDULE: return "SCHEDULE";
        default:                       return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

const char* CT10_sessionTypeToString(EN_CT10_session_type_t t) {
    switch (t) {
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE:     return "NONE";
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST: return "PLAYLIST";
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET:   return "PRESET";
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED:    return "FIXED";
        default:                       return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

// ======================================================
// decideSession — 5단 우선순위
// ======================================================
ST_CT10_Decision_t CL_CT10_ControlManager::decideSession() {
    ST_CT10_Decision_t d = {};
    d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_IDLE;
    d.session.type = EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE;
    d.wantSimStop = false;

    // [1] AutoOff 래치
    if (_autoOffLatched) {
        d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_AUTOOFF_STOPPED;
        d.wantSimStop = true;
        return d;
    }

    // [2] Manual Session 활성?
    if (_session.type != EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE &&
        _session.trigger == EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL &&
        (_session.state == EN_SESSION_ACTIVE || _session.state == EN_SESSION_PAUSED)) {

        // Pause 상태: 상태 유지, 실행 안 함
        if (_session.state == EN_SESSION_PAUSED) {
            d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING;
            d.session = _session;
            d.wantSimStop = true;   // 팬 정지 유지
            return d;
        }

        // PIR 재실 검사 (PLAYLIST, PRESET만, FIXED는 스킵)
        if (_session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST) {
            int pIdx = findProfileById(_session.payload.playlist.profileId);
            if (pIdx >= 0) {
                const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx];
                if (isMotionBlocked(prof.motion)) {
                    d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_MOTION_BLOCKED;
                    d.session = _session;
                    d.wantSimStop = true;
                    return d;
                }
            }
        }

        d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING;
        d.session = _session;
        return d;
    }

    // [3] 시간 유효성
    if (!CL_TM10_TimeManager::isTimeValid()) {
        d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_TIME_INVALID;
        d.wantSimStop = true;
        return d;
    }

    // [4] Schedule
    int schIdx = findActiveScheduleIndex(true);
    if (schIdx >= 0) {
        const auto& sch = g_A20_config_root.schedules->items[schIdx];
        if (sch.profileId != 0) {
            int pIdx = findProfileById(sch.profileId);
            if (pIdx >= 0) {
                const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx];
                if (prof.enabled && prof.stepCount > 0) {
                    // PIR 재실
                    if (isMotionBlocked(prof.motion)) {
                        d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_MOTION_BLOCKED;
                        d.session.type = EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST;
                        d.session.trigger = EN_CT10_TRIGGER_SCHEDULE;
                        d.session.payload.playlist.profileId = sch.profileId;
                        d.session.payload.playlist.profileNo = prof.profileNo;
                        d.session.activeSchId = sch.schId;
                        d.session.activeSchNo = sch.schNo;
                        d.wantSimStop = true;
                        return d;
                    }

                    // Schedule Session 자동 시작
                    d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING;
                    d.session.type = EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST;
                    d.session.trigger = EN_CT10_TRIGGER_SCHEDULE;
                    d.session.state = EN_SESSION_ACTIVE;
                    d.session.startedAt = millis();
                    d.session.currentStepStartMs = millis();
                    d.session.payload.playlist.profileId = sch.profileId;
                    d.session.payload.playlist.profileNo = prof.profileNo;
                    d.session.payload.playlist.totalSteps = prof.stepCount;
                    d.session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx = 0;
                    d.session.payload.playlist.loopCount = 0;
                    d.session.activeSchId = sch.schId;
                    d.session.activeSchNo = sch.schNo;
                    return d;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // [5] IDLE
    d.state = EN_CT10_STATE_IDLE;
    d.wantSimStop = true;
    return d;
}

// ======================================================
// applyDecision
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::applyDecision(const ST_CT10_Decision_t& d) {
    bool stateChanged = (_runCtx.state != d.state);
    bool sourceChanged = (_session.type != d.session.type ||
                          _session.trigger != d.session.trigger ||
                          _session.payload.playlist.profileId != d.session.payload.playlist.profileId);

    // 세션 스왑 (Schedule 자동 시작)
    if (d.session.type != EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE &&
        d.session.trigger == EN_CT10_TRIGGER_SCHEDULE &&
        _session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE) {
        _session = d.session;
        int pIdx = findProfileById(_session.payload.playlist.profileId);
        if (pIdx >= 0) {
            initAutoOffFromProfile(g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx]);
        }
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[CT10] Schedule Session started (schNo=%u, profileNo=%u)",
            _session.activeSchNo, _session.payload.playlist.profileNo);
    }

    // Schedule 이탈 시 세션 해제
    if (d.session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE &&
        _session.trigger == EN_CT10_TRIGGER_SCHEDULE) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[CT10] Schedule Session ended (schNo=%u)", _session.activeSchNo);
        memset(&_session, 0, sizeof(_session));
        _session.type = EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE;
    }

    if (d.wantSimStop && sim.active) sim.stop();

    _runCtx.state          = d.state;
    _runCtx.sessionType    = _session.type;
    _runCtx.trigger        = _session.trigger;
    _runCtx.sessionState   = _session.state;
    _runCtx.lastDecisionMs = millis();

    if (stateChanged || sourceChanged) {
        _runCtx.lastStateChangeMs = _runCtx.lastDecisionMs;
        markDirty("state");
        markDirty("metrics");
        markDirty("summary");

        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[CT10] State=%s sessionType=%s trigger=%s",
            CT10_stateToString(_runCtx.state),
            CT10_sessionTypeToString(_runCtx.sessionType),
            CT10_triggerToString(_runCtx.trigger));
    }
}
```

5. CT10_Ctl_Session_0203.cpp — tickSession + tickPlaylist

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// tickLoop (메인 루프 진입점)
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::tickLoop() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return;
    if (!active || !pwm) return;

    uint32_t now = millis();
    if (now - _lastTickMs < S_TICK_MIN_INTERVAL_MS) return;
    _lastTickMs = now;

    // FSM 결정 → 적용
    ST_CT10_Decision_t d = decideSession();
    applyDecision(d);

    // Pending Schedule 정보 갱신 (Manual 중일 때)
    updatePendingScheduleInfo();

    // 상태별 실행
    if (_runCtx.state == EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING) {
        tickSession();
    }
}

// ======================================================
// tickSession (type별 분기)
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::tickSession() {
    // Pause 상태: 팬 정지 유지, 시간 진행 없음
    if (_session.state == EN_SESSION_PAUSED) {
        return;
    }

    switch (_session.type) {
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST: tickPlaylist(); break;
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET:   tickPreset();   break;
        case EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED:    tickFixed();    break;
        default: break;
    }
}

// ======================================================
// tickPlaylist — Step 시퀀스
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::tickPlaylist() {
    int pIdx = findProfileById(_session.payload.playlist.profileId);
    if (pIdx < 0) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR,
            "[CT10] tickPlaylist: profileId=%u not found",
            _session.payload.playlist.profileId);
        stopSession();
        return;
    }
    const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx];

    // AutoOff 검사
    if (checkAutoOff()) {
        onAutoOffTriggered();
        return;
    }

    // 첫 Step 진입 (applyDecision 직후)
    if (_session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx == 0 &&
        _session.currentStepStartMs == _session.startedAt) {
        applyStep(prof.steps[0]);
        return;
    }

    // Step 진행
    bool advanced = tickStepSequence(prof,
        _session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx,
        _session.payload.playlist.loopCount);

    if (advanced) {
        // 완료
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[CT10] Playlist completed (profileNo=%u)",
            _session.payload.playlist.profileNo);
        stopSession();
        return;
    }

    // Step 전환 검사
    const auto& step = prof.steps[_session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx];
    uint32_t elapsed = sessionElapsedMs();
    uint32_t stepElapsed = elapsed - (_session.currentStepStartMs - _session.startedAt);

    if (stepElapsed >= (uint32_t)step.durationMin * 60000UL) {
        _session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx++;
        if (_session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx >= prof.stepCount) {
            if (prof.repeat &&
                (prof.repeatCount == 0 ||
                 ++_session.payload.playlist.loopCount < prof.repeatCount)) {
                _session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx = 0;
            } else {
                stopSession();
                return;
            }
        }
        _session.currentStepStartMs = millis();
        applyStep(prof.steps[_session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx]);
        emitStepChange();
    }

    // sim tick
    if (sim.active) sim.tick();
}

// ======================================================
// tickStepSequence — profile/steps 진행만 체크
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::tickStepSequence(const ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& prof,
                                              uint8_t& currentIdx, uint8_t& loopCount) {
    (void)prof; (void)currentIdx; (void)loopCount;
    return false;   // tickPlaylist 내부에서 처리
}

// ======================================================
// tickPreset
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::tickPreset() {
    if (checkAutoOff()) { onAutoOffTriggered(); return; }

    // 첫 진입
    if (_session.startedAt == _session.currentStepStartMs) {
        applyPreset(_session.payload.preset.presetCode,
                    _session.payload.preset.styleCode,
                    &_session.payload.preset.override);
    }

    if (sim.active) sim.tick();
}

// ======================================================
// tickFixed
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::tickFixed() {
    if (checkAutoOff()) { onAutoOffTriggered(); return; }

    if (_session.startedAt == _session.currentStepStartMs) {
        applyFixed(_session.payload.fixed.percent);
    }
}

// ======================================================
// applyStep / applyPreset / applyFixed / applyRest
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::applyStep(const ST_A20_Step_t& step) {
    switch (step.type) {
        case EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_PRESET:
            applyPreset(step.presetCode, step.styleCode, &step.override);
            break;
        case EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_FIXED:
            applyFixed(step.fixedSpeed);
            break;
        case EN_A20_STEP_TYPE_REST:
            applyRest();
            break;
        default: break;
    }
    markDirty("state");
    markDirty("chart");
}

void CL_CT10_ControlManager::applyPreset(const char* pc, const char* sc,
                                          const ST_A20_SegmentOverride_t* ovr) {
    if (!g_A20_config_root.windDict) return;

    ST_A20_ResolvedWind_t resolved;
    memset(&resolved, 0, sizeof(resolved));
    bool ok = S20_resolveWindParams(*g_A20_config_root.windDict, pc, sc, ovr, resolved);
    if (ok && resolved.valid) {
        sim.applyResolvedWind(resolved);
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[CT10] applyPreset: %s / %s", pc, sc);
    } else {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_WARN,
            "[CT10] applyPreset resolve failed: %s / %s", pc, sc);
    }
}

void CL_CT10_ControlManager::applyFixed(float percent) {
    if (sim.active) sim.stop();
    if (pwm) pwm->setDutyPercent(percent);
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[CT10] applyFixed: %.1f%%", percent);
}

void CL_CT10_ControlManager::applyRest() {
    if (sim.active) sim.stop();
    if (pwm) pwm->setDutyPercent(0);
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[CT10] applyRest (fan off)");
}

// ======================================================
// 유틸
// ======================================================
uint32_t CL_CT10_ControlManager::sessionElapsedMs() const {
    uint32_t now = millis();
    uint32_t elapsed = now - _session.startedAt - _session.pausedAccumMs;
    return elapsed;
}

void CL_CT10_ControlManager::emitStepChange() {
    markDirty("state");
    markDirty("summary");
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[CT10] Step changed → %u/%u (loop=%u)",
        _session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx + 1,
        _session.payload.playlist.totalSteps,
        _session.payload.playlist.loopCount);
}
```

6. CT10_Ctl_Pause_0203.cpp — Pause / Resume (5분/30분 정책)

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// pauseSession
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::pauseSession() {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE) return false;
    if (self._session.state != EN_SESSION_ACTIVE) return false;

    self._session.state      = EN_SESSION_PAUSED;
    self._session.pausedAtMs = millis();

    // 팬 정지
    self.sim.stop();
    if (self.pwm) self.pwm->setDutyPercent(0);

    self.markDirty("state");
    self.markDirty("metrics");
    self.markDirty("summary");

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[CT10] pauseSession: type=%u, elapsed=%ums",
        (unsigned)self._session.type, self.sessionElapsedMs());
    return true;
}

// ======================================================
// resumeSession (3구간 정책)
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::resumeSession() {
    auto& self = instance();
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return false;

    if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE) return false;
    if (self._session.state != EN_SESSION_PAUSED) return false;

    uint32_t pauseDurMs = millis() - self._session.pausedAtMs;

    enum { MODE_CONTINUE, MODE_STEP_RESET, MODE_RESTART } mode;

    if (pauseDurMs < S_PAUSE_CONTINUE_MS) {
        // < 5분: 이어서 재생
        self._session.pausedAccumMs += pauseDurMs;
        mode = MODE_CONTINUE;
    } else if (pauseDurMs < S_PAUSE_STEP_RESET_MS) {
        // 5~30분: 현재 Step 처음부터
        self._session.pausedAccumMs += pauseDurMs;
        self._session.currentStepStartMs = millis();
        if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST) {
            int pIdx = self.findProfileById(self._session.payload.playlist.profileId);
            if (pIdx >= 0) {
                const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx];
                self.applyStep(prof.steps[self._session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx]);
            }
        } else if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET) {
            self.applyPreset(self._session.payload.preset.presetCode,
                             self._session.payload.preset.styleCode,
                             &self._session.payload.preset.override);
        } else if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED) {
            self.applyFixed(self._session.payload.fixed.percent);
        }
        mode = MODE_STEP_RESET;
    } else {
        // > 30분: 처음부터
        self._session.pausedAccumMs = 0;
        self._session.startedAt = millis();
        self._session.currentStepStartMs = millis();

        if (self._session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST) {
            self._session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx = 0;
            self._session.payload.playlist.loopCount = 0;
            int pIdx = self.findProfileById(self._session.payload.playlist.profileId);
            if (pIdx >= 0) {
                const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx];
                self.applyStep(prof.steps[0]);
            }
        }
        mode = MODE_RESTART;
    }

    self._session.pausedAtMs = 0;
    self._session.state      = EN_SESSION_ACTIVE;

    self.markDirty("state");
    self.markDirty("metrics");
    self.markDirty("summary");

    const char* modeStr = mode == MODE_CONTINUE ? "CONTINUE"
                        : mode == MODE_STEP_RESET ? "STEP_RESET"
                                                  : "RESTART";
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
        "[CT10] resumeSession: pauseDur=%ums → mode=%s",
        pauseDurMs, modeStr);
    return true;
}
```

7. CT10_Ctl_AutoOff_0203.cpp — AutoOff 통합

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// initAutoOffFromProfile
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::initAutoOffFromProfile(const ST_A20_ProfileItem_t& p) {
    memset(&_runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed, 0, sizeof(uint32_t) * 1);
    _runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed    = false;
    _runCtx.autoOffTimerMinutes  = 0;
    _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeEnabled = false;
    _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeMinutes = 0;
    _runCtx.autoOffOffTempEnabled = false;
    _runCtx.autoOffOffTemp       = 0.0f;

    _persistOffTimeLastYday = -1;

    if (p.autoOff.timer.enabled && p.autoOff.timer.minutes > 0) {
        _runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed   = true;
        _runCtx.autoOffTimerStartMs = millis();
        _runCtx.autoOffTimerMinutes = p.autoOff.timer.minutes;
    }
    if (p.autoOff.offTime.enabled) {
        _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeEnabled = true;
        _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeMinutes =
            A40_parseHHMMtoMin_24h(p.autoOff.offTime.time);
    }
    if (p.autoOff.offTemp.enabled) {
        _runCtx.autoOffOffTempEnabled = true;
        _runCtx.autoOffOffTemp        = p.autoOff.offTemp.temp;
    }
}

// ======================================================
// checkAutoOff
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::checkAutoOff() {
    if (!_runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed &&
        !_runCtx.autoOffOffTimeEnabled &&
        !_runCtx.autoOffOffTempEnabled) return false;

    // Timer
    if (_runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed && _runCtx.autoOffTimerMinutes > 0) {
        uint32_t elapsedMin =
            (millis() - _runCtx.autoOffTimerStartMs) / 60000UL;
        if (elapsedMin >= _runCtx.autoOffTimerMinutes) {
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
                "[CT10] AutoOff timer triggered (%umin)", _runCtx.autoOffTimerMinutes);
            return true;
        }
    }

    // offTime
    if (_runCtx.autoOffOffTimeEnabled) {
        struct tm tm;
        if (CL_TM10_TimeManager::getLocalTime(tm)) {
            uint16_t curMin = (uint16_t)tm.tm_hour * 60 + tm.tm_min;
            if (curMin >= _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeMinutes &&
                _persistOffTimeLastYday != tm.tm_yday) {
                _persistOffTimeLastYday = tm.tm_yday;
                CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
                    "[CT10] AutoOff offTime triggered (yday=%d)", tm.tm_yday);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }

    // offTemp
    if (_runCtx.autoOffOffTempEnabled) {
        extern float CT10_readTempC();
        float t = CT10_readTempC();
        if (t >= _runCtx.autoOffOffTemp) {
            CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
                "[CT10] AutoOff temp triggered (%.1f >= %.1f)",
                t, _runCtx.autoOffOffTemp);
            return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

// ======================================================
// onAutoOffTriggered
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::onAutoOffTriggered() {
    if (sim.active) sim.stop();
    if (pwm) pwm->setDutyPercent(0);

    // Session 정지
    memset(&_session, 0, sizeof(_session));
    _session.type  = EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE;
    _session.state = EN_SESSION_COMPLETED;

    // AutoOff 런타임 초기화
    _runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed     = false;
    _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeEnabled = false;
    _runCtx.autoOffOffTempEnabled = false;
    _persistOffTimeLastYday       = -1;

    // 래치 + 이벤트 상태
    _autoOffLatched = true;
    _runCtx.state = EN_CT10_STATE_AUTOOFF_STOPPED;
    _runCtx.lastStateChangeMs = millis();
    _runCtx.stateHoldUntilMs  = millis() + S_EVENT_HOLD_MS;

    // NVS 해제
    extern class CL_N10_NvsManager;
    // (N10_NvsManager::setManualSession(0, false) 호출은 헤더 include 필요)

    markDirty("state");
    markDirty("metrics");
    markDirty("summary");

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[CT10] AutoOff → STOPPED (latched)");
}

// ======================================================
// ackEventState
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::ackEventState() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return;

    if (_runCtx.state != EN_CT10_STATE_AUTOOFF_STOPPED) return;

    _autoOffLatched = false;
    _runCtx.stateAckRequired = false;
    _runCtx.stateHoldUntilMs = 0;

    markDirty("state");
    markDirty("summary");
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[CT10] AutoOff ack → released");
}
```
