8. CT10_Ctl_Basic_0203.cpp — Motion / Schedule 인덱스 / DI

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"
#include <DHT.h>

// ======================================================
// DHT 캐시 (파일 스코프)
// ======================================================
namespace {
struct DhtCache {
    DHT*     dht = nullptr;
    int16_t  pin = -1;
    uint32_t lastReadMs = 0;
    float    temp = 24.0f;
    float    hum  = 55.0f;
};
DhtCache s_dht;

void readDhtIfNeeded() {
    if (!g_A20_config_root.system) return;
    const auto& cfg = g_A20_config_root.system->hw.tempHum;
    if (!cfg.enabled) return;

    int16_t pin = (cfg.pin > 0) ? cfg.pin : 17;
    if (!s_dht.dht) {
        s_dht.pin = pin;
        s_dht.dht = new DHT(pin, DHT22);
        s_dht.dht->begin();
    }
    uint32_t now = millis();
    if (now - s_dht.lastReadMs < 2000) return;
    s_dht.lastReadMs = now;

    float t = s_dht.dht->readTemperature();
    float h = s_dht.dht->readHumidity();
    if (!isnan(t)) s_dht.temp = t;
    if (!isnan(h)) s_dht.hum  = h;
}
}

float CT10_readTempC() {
    readDhtIfNeeded();
    return s_dht.temp;
}
float CT10_readHumidity() {
    readDhtIfNeeded();
    return s_dht.hum;
}

// ======================================================
// Motion
// ======================================================
bool CL_CT10_ControlManager::isMotionBlocked(const ST_A20_Motion_t& cfg) {
    if (!motion) return false;
    if (!cfg.pir.enabled) return false;
    return !motion->isActive();
}

// ======================================================
// findActiveScheduleIndex
// ======================================================
int CL_CT10_ControlManager::findActiveScheduleIndex(bool allowOverlap) {
    if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules) return -1;
    auto& cfg = *g_A20_config_root.schedules;
    if (cfg.count == 0) return -1;

    struct tm tm;
    if (!CL_TM10_TimeManager::getLocalTime(tm)) return -1;

    uint8_t today = (tm.tm_wday == 0) ? 6 : (uint8_t)(tm.tm_wday - 1);
    uint8_t yday  = (today == 0) ? 6 : (uint8_t)(today - 1);
    uint16_t curMin = (uint16_t)tm.tm_hour * 60 + tm.tm_min;

    int bestIdx = -1;
    uint16_t bestNo = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < (int)cfg.count; i++) {
        const auto& s = cfg.items[i];
        if (!s.enabled || s.profileId == 0) continue;

        uint16_t start = A40_parseHHMMtoMin_24h(s.period.startTime);
        uint16_t end   = A40_parseHHMMtoMin_24h(s.period.endTime);

        bool match = false;
        if (start == end) {
            match = s.period.days[today];
        } else if (start < end) {
            if (s.period.days[today])
                match = (curMin >= start && curMin < end);
        } else {
            if (curMin >= start) match = s.period.days[today];
            else if (curMin < end) match = s.period.days[yday];
        }
        if (!match) continue;

        if (!allowOverlap) return i;
        if (bestIdx < 0 || s.schNo > bestNo) {
            bestIdx = i;
            bestNo = s.schNo;
        }
    }
    return bestIdx;
}

// ======================================================
// findProfileById
// ======================================================
int CL_CT10_ControlManager::findProfileById(uint8_t profileId) {
    if (!g_A20_config_root.profiles || profileId == 0) return -1;
    auto& cfg = *g_A20_config_root.profiles;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cfg.count; i++) {
        if (cfg.items[i].profileId == profileId) return (int)i;
    }
    return -1;
}

// ======================================================
// updatePendingScheduleInfo
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::updatePendingScheduleInfo() {
    memset(&_runCtx.pendingScheduleActive, 0, sizeof(_runCtx.pendingScheduleActive));
    _runCtx.pendingScheduleActive = false;

    if (_session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE) return;
    if (_session.trigger != EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL) return;

    if (!g_A20_config_root.schedules) return;
    auto& cfg = *g_A20_config_root.schedules;

    struct tm tm;
    if (!CL_TM10_TimeManager::getLocalTime(tm)) return;

    uint8_t today = (tm.tm_wday == 0) ? 6 : (uint8_t)(tm.tm_wday - 1);
    uint16_t curMin = (uint16_t)tm.tm_hour * 60 + tm.tm_min;

    uint16_t nearest = 0xFFFF;
    int nearestIdx = -1;

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < cfg.count; i++) {
        const auto& s = cfg.items[i];
        if (!s.enabled || s.profileId == 0) continue;
        if (!s.period.days[today]) continue;
        uint16_t start = A40_parseHHMMtoMin_24h(s.period.startTime);
        if (start > curMin && (start - curMin) < nearest) {
            nearest = start - curMin;
            nearestIdx = i;
        }
    }

    if (nearestIdx >= 0) {
        const auto& s = cfg.items[nearestIdx];
        _runCtx.pendingScheduleActive = true;
        _runCtx.pendingSchId = s.schId;
        _runCtx.pendingSchNo = s.schNo;
        A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(_runCtx.pendingSchName,
            s.name, sizeof(_runCtx.pendingSchName));
        A40_ComFunc::copyStr2Buffer_safe(_runCtx.pendingSchStart,
            s.period.startTime, sizeof(_runCtx.pendingSchStart));
    }
}

// ======================================================
// DI 계산
// ======================================================
float CL_CT10_ControlManager::getDiscomfortIndex() {
    float t = CT10_readTempC();
    float h = CT10_readHumidity();
    return 0.81f * t + 0.01f * h * (0.99f * t - 14.3f) + 46.3f;
}

// (헤더에 추가 필요: getDiscomfortIndex)
```

9. N10_NvsManager_0203.h/.cpp — NVS 확장

```cpp
// N10_NvsManager_0203.h
#pragma once
#include <Preferences.h>
#include "A20_Const_0203.h"
#include "D10_Logger_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"

typedef struct {
    uint8_t  runMode;
    uint8_t  runSource;
    int16_t  lastScheduleNo;
    int16_t  lastUserProfileNo;
    bool     manualActive;      // ★ v0203
    int16_t  manualProfileNo;   // ★ v0203
    bool     manualOvrSchedule; // ★ v0203
} ST_N10_RuntimeState_t;

class CL_N10_NvsManager {
  public:
    static constexpr const char* G_N10_NS_RUNTIME      = "SNW_RUN";
    static constexpr uint32_t    G_N10_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 10000;

    // ★ v0203 신규 키
    static constexpr const char* K_MAN_ACTIVE    = "man_act";      // uint8
    static constexpr const char* K_MAN_PROFILE   = "man_prof";     // int16
    static constexpr const char* K_MAN_OVR_SCH   = "man_ovr_sch";  // uint8

    static bool begin();
    static void tick();
    static void setManualSession(int16_t profileNo, bool active);
    static int16_t getManualProfileNo();
    static bool    isManualActive();
    static void    setManualOverrideSchedule(bool v);
    static bool    getManualOverrideSchedule();
    static void    clearAll();

  private:
    static Preferences s_prefs;
    static SemaphoreHandle_t s_mutex;
    static ST_N10_RuntimeState_t s_state;
    static bool s_dirty;
};
```

```cpp
// N10_NvsManager_0203.cpp
#include "N10_NvsManager_0203.h"
#include <nvs.h>

Preferences CL_N10_NvsManager::s_prefs;
SemaphoreHandle_t CL_N10_NvsManager::s_mutex = nullptr;
ST_N10_RuntimeState_t CL_N10_NvsManager::s_state = {};
bool CL_N10_NvsManager::s_dirty = false;

bool CL_N10_NvsManager::begin() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return false;
    if (!s_prefs.begin(G_N10_NS_RUNTIME, false)) return false;

    s_state.runMode            = s_prefs.getUChar("run_mode", 0);
    s_state.runSource          = s_prefs.getUChar("run_src", 0);
    s_state.lastScheduleNo     = s_prefs.getShort("sched_no", -1);
    s_state.lastUserProfileNo  = s_prefs.getShort("uprof_no", -1);
    s_state.manualActive       = s_prefs.getUChar(K_MAN_ACTIVE, 0) != 0;
    s_state.manualProfileNo    = s_prefs.getShort(K_MAN_PROFILE, 0);
    s_state.manualOvrSchedule  = s_prefs.getUChar(K_MAN_OVR_SCH, 0) != 0;
    return true;
}

void CL_N10_NvsManager::setManualSession(int16_t profileNo, bool active) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return;
    s_state.manualProfileNo = profileNo;
    s_state.manualActive    = active;
    s_dirty = true;
}

int16_t CL_N10_NvsManager::getManualProfileNo() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return 0;
    return s_state.manualProfileNo;
}

bool CL_N10_NvsManager::isManualActive() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return false;
    return s_state.manualActive;
}

void CL_N10_NvsManager::setManualOverrideSchedule(bool v) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return;
    s_state.manualOvrSchedule = v;
    s_dirty = true;
}

bool CL_N10_NvsManager::getManualOverrideSchedule() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return false;
    return s_state.manualOvrSchedule;
}

void CL_N10_NvsManager::tick() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired() || !s_dirty) return;

    nvs_handle_t h;
    if (nvs_open(G_N10_NS_RUNTIME, NVS_READWRITE, &h) != ESP_OK) return;

    nvs_set_u8 (h, K_MAN_ACTIVE,  s_state.manualActive ? 1 : 0);
    nvs_set_i16(h, K_MAN_PROFILE, s_state.manualProfileNo);
    nvs_set_u8 (h, K_MAN_OVR_SCH,s_state.manualOvrSchedule ? 1 : 0);
    nvs_commit(h);
    nvs_close(h);

    s_dirty = false;
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_DEBUG, "[N10] NVS flushed");
}

void CL_N10_NvsManager::clearAll() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore g(s_mutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!g.isAcquired()) return;
    s_prefs.clear();
    memset(&s_state, 0, sizeof(s_state));
    s_dirty = true;
}
```

10. CT10_Ctl_IOWS_0203.cpp — Session Export

```cpp
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"

static JsonDocument s_doc_state;

void CL_CT10_ControlManager::exportStateJson_v02(JsonDocument& doc) {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return;

    JsonObject root = doc.to<JsonObject>();
    JsonObject ctl  = A40_ComFunc::Json_ensureObject(root["control"]);

    ctl["state"]      = CT10_stateToString(_runCtx.state);
    ctl["stateCode"]  = (uint8_t)_runCtx.state;
    ctl["active"]     = (_runCtx.state == EN_CT10_STATE_RUNNING);
    ctl["lastDecisionMs"]    = _runCtx.lastDecisionMs;
    ctl["lastStateChangeMs"] = _runCtx.lastStateChangeMs;

    // Session (핵심)
    JsonObject sess = A40_ComFunc::Json_ensureObject(ctl["session"]);
    sess["type"]    = CT10_sessionTypeToString(_session.type);
    sess["trigger"] = CT10_triggerToString(_session.trigger);
    sess["state"]   = (_session.state == EN_SESSION_ACTIVE)   ? "ACTIVE"
                    : (_session.state == EN_SESSION_PAUSED)   ? "PAUSED"
                                                              : "COMPLETED";

    if (_session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST) {
        int pIdx = findProfileById(_session.payload.playlist.profileId);
        sess["profileId"]   = _session.payload.playlist.profileId;
        sess["profileNo"]   = _session.payload.playlist.profileNo;
        sess["profileName"] = (pIdx >= 0) ? g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx].name : "";
        sess["currentStep"] = _session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx + 1;
        sess["totalSteps"]  = _session.payload.playlist.totalSteps;
        sess["loopCount"]   = _session.payload.playlist.loopCount;

        uint32_t elapsed = sessionElapsedMs();
        uint32_t stepStart = _session.currentStepStartMs - _session.startedAt;
        uint32_t stepElapsed = elapsed - stepStart;
        if (pIdx >= 0) {
            const auto& prof = g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[pIdx];
            uint8_t idx = _session.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx;
            if (idx < prof.stepCount) {
                uint32_t durMs = (uint32_t)prof.steps[idx].durationMin * 60000UL;
                sess["stepRemainSec"] = (durMs > stepElapsed)
                    ? (durMs - stepElapsed) / 1000 : 0;
            }
        }
    } else if (_session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET) {
        sess["presetCode"] = _session.payload.preset.presetCode;
        sess["styleCode"]  = _session.payload.preset.styleCode;
    } else if (_session.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED) {
        sess["percent"] = _session.payload.fixed.percent;
    }

    sess["pausedAccumMs"] = _session.pausedAccumMs;

    // AutoOff
    JsonObject ao = A40_ComFunc::Json_ensureObject(ctl["autoOffRt"]);
    ao["timerArmed"]     = _runCtx.autoOffTimerArmed;
    ao["timerMinutes"]   = _runCtx.autoOffTimerMinutes;
    ao["offTimeEnabled"] = _runCtx.autoOffOffTimeEnabled;
    ao["offTempEnabled"] = _runCtx.autoOffOffTempEnabled;

    // Pending Schedule
    JsonObject ps = A40_ComFunc::Json_ensureObject(ctl["pendingSchedule"]);
    ps["active"] = _runCtx.pendingScheduleActive;
    if (_runCtx.pendingScheduleActive) {
        ps["schId"]      = _runCtx.pendingSchId;
        ps["schNo"]      = _runCtx.pendingSchNo;
        ps["name"]       = _runCtx.pendingSchName;
        ps["startTime"]  = _runCtx.pendingSchStart;
        ps["inactiveDueToManual"] = true;
    }

    // Sensor
    JsonObject sensor = A40_ComFunc::Json_ensureObject(ctl["sensor"]);
    sensor["tempC"]        = CT10_readTempC();
    sensor["humidity"]     = CT10_readHumidity();
    sensor["motionActive"] = motion ? motion->isActive() : false;

    // 시스템 시각
    JsonObject sys = A40_ComFunc::Json_ensureObject(ctl["system"]);
    sys["uptimeMs"]   = (uint32_t)millis();
    sys["epochNowMs"] = CL_TM10_TimeManager::isTimeValid()
        ? (uint64_t)time(nullptr) * 1000ULL : 0ULL;

    // sim 병합
    sim.toJson(doc);
}

// ======================================================
// Dirty
// ======================================================
void CL_CT10_ControlManager::markDirty(const char* key) {
    if (!key || !key[0]) return;
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return;

    if (strcmp(key, "state") == 0)        _dirtyState   = true;
    else if (strcmp(key, "metrics") == 0) _dirtyMetrics = true;
    else if (strcmp(key, "chart") == 0)   _dirtyChart   = true;
    else if (strcmp(key, "summary") == 0) _dirtySummary = true;
}

uint8_t CL_CT10_ControlManager::consumeAllDirty() {
    CL_A40_MutexGuard_Semaphore v_guard(s_stateMutex, G_A40_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_100, __func__);
    if (!v_guard.isAcquired()) return 0;

    uint8_t ret = 0;
    if (_dirtyState)   { ret |= 0x01; _dirtyState   = false; }
    if (_dirtyMetrics) { ret |= 0x02; _dirtyMetrics = false; }
    if (_dirtyChart)   { ret |= 0x04; _dirtyChart   = false; }
    if (_dirtySummary) { ret |= 0x08; _dirtySummary = false; }
    return ret;
}
```

11. A00_Main_0203.cpp — 부팅 시퀀스

```cpp
#include "A00_Main_0203.h"
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include "N10_NvsManager_0203.h"
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"

extern CL_P10_PWM g_P10_pwm;
extern AsyncWebServer g_A00_server;

void A00_init() {
    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "=== SNW Boot v0203 ===");

    if (!LittleFS.begin(true)) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_ERROR, "[FS] mount failed");
    }

    if (!CL_C10_ConfigManager::begin()) return;
    if (!CL_C10_ConfigManager::loadAll(g_A20_config_root)) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_WARN, "[C10] loadAll partial");
    }

    if (!CL_N10_NvsManager::begin()) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_WARN, "[N10] NVS begin failed");
    }

    // ★ Manual Session 복원
    int16_t manProfNo = CL_N10_NvsManager::getManualProfileNo();
    if (manProfNo > 0 && g_A20_config_root.profiles) {
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < g_A20_config_root.profiles->count; i++) {
            if (g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[i].profileNo == (uint16_t)manProfNo) {
                CL_CT10_ControlManager::startPlaylistManual(
                    g_A20_config_root.profiles->items[i].profileId);
                CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
                    "[BOOT] Manual Session restored: profileNo=%u", manProfNo);
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    // 하드웨어
    g_P10_pwm.P10_begin(*g_A20_config_root.system);

    // CT10
    CL_CT10_ControlManager::begin();
    CL_CT10_ControlManager::setMotion(g_M10_motionLogic);

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[A00] Init complete");
}

void A00_run() {
    esp_task_wdt_reset();
    CL_CT10_ControlManager::tick();
    if (g_M10_motionLogic) g_M10_motionLogic->tick();
    CL_N10_NvsManager::tick();
    CL_C10_ConfigManager::processPendingFree();
    delay(A20_Const::G_A00_LOOP_DELAY_MS);
}
```

---

12. Stage 2 검증 시나리오 (Serial CLI)

CLI 명령 예시 (임시 디버그용)

```cpp
// A00_Main_0203.cpp에 임시 추가 (Stage 3에서 삭제)
void handleSerialCommand() {
    if (!Serial.available()) return;
    String cmd = Serial.readStringUntil('\n');
    cmd.trim();

    if (cmd.startsWith("session start playlist ")) {
        uint8_t pid = cmd.substring(23).toInt();
        bool ok = CL_CT10_ControlManager::startPlaylistManual(pid);
        Serial.printf("[CLI] start playlist %u → %d\n", pid, ok);
    }
    else if (cmd == "session pause") {
        Serial.printf("[CLI] pause → %d\n", CL_CT10_ControlManager::pauseSession());
    }
    else if (cmd == "session resume") {
        Serial.printf("[CLI] resume → %d\n", CL_CT10_ControlManager::resumeSession());
    }
    else if (cmd == "session stop") {
        Serial.printf("[CLI] stop → %d\n", CL_CT10_ControlManager::stopSession());
    }
    else if (cmd == "session status") {
        auto s = CL_CT10_ControlManager::getSessionSnapshot();
        Serial.printf("[CLI] type=%u trigger=%u state=%u\n",
            (unsigned)s.type, (unsigned)s.trigger, (unsigned)s.state);
    }
    else if (cmd == "session fixed 60") {
        Serial.printf("[CLI] fixed 60%% → %d\n",
            CL_CT10_ControlManager::startFixedManual(60));
    }
}
```

검증 체크리스트 (Stage 2 완료 조건)

A. Session 시작/종료

☐ session start playlist 1 → 로그 "startSession: type=1 trigger=1"
☐ Playlist Step 1 → Step 2 → Step 3 순서 자동 진행
☐ Step 3 완료 후 repeatCount=2 → Playlist 재시작 (loopCount++)
☐ repeatCount 소진 → 자동 종료 (로그 "Playlist completed")
☐ session stop → 즉시 종료
☐ session fixed 60 → 60% PWM 고정
☐ session start preset OCEAN BALANCE → 프리셋 재생

B. Pause/Resume (핵심)

☐ session pause → 팬 정지, 상태 PAUSED
☐ 즉시 session resume (< 5분) → 이어서 재생 (남은 시간 유지)
☐ 6분 대기 후 session resume → 현재 Step 처음부터
☐ 40분 대기 후 session resume → Playlist Step 0부터

C. NVS 영속화

☐ session start playlist 1 → 재부팅 → Session 자동 복원
☐ session fixed 60 → 재부팅 → IDLE (복원 안 함)
☐ session stop → 재부팅 → IDLE

D. FSM 우선순위

☐ Schedule Session (자동) 중 → session start playlist 2 → Manual로 교체
☐ Manual 중 Schedule 시간 진입 → Manual 유지 (로그 확인)
☐ Manual 종료 → Schedule 자동 재개

E. AutoOff

☐ Profile에 autoOff.timer.minutes=1 설정 → 1분 후 자동 STOPPED
☐ AutoOff 래치 중 session start fixed 60 → 래치 해제 + 재생
☐ AutoOff 래치 상태에서 stopSession → 래치 유지

F. Motion (PIR)

☐ PIR 감지 OFF 상태에서 Playlist 시작 → MOTION_BLOCKED
☐ PIR 감지 ON → RUNNING으로 자동 복귀

G. 참조 무결성

☐ Schedule Session 재생 중 deleteProfile → Session 정지 (profileId 못 찾음)

---

13. Stage 2 완료 후 상태

· 완전한 Session 라이프사이클 (start/pause/resume/stop)

2. 백엔드 — REST API

2.1 W10_Web_0203.h — 라우트 등록

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <ESPAsyncWebServer.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>

// ======================================================
// 공용 유틸
// ======================================================
bool W10_checkApiKey(AsyncWebServerRequest* req);
bool W10_parseJsonBody(AsyncWebServerRequest* req, JsonDocument& doc,
                       uint8_t* buf, size_t bufSize);
void W10_sendJson(AsyncWebServerRequest* req, int code, const JsonDocument& doc);

// ======================================================
// v0203 Web API
// ======================================================
class CL_W10_WebAPI {
  public:
    static void begin(AsyncWebServer& server);

    // 라우트 등록 (각 .cpp)
    static void registerSessionRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server);
    static void registerProfileRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server);
    static void registerScheduleRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server);
    static void registerStateRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server);
    static void registerWsRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server);
    static void registerStaticRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server);
};
```

2.2 W10_Web_0203.cpp — 서버 begin

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "D10_Logger_0203.h"
#include "A20_Const_0203.h"

void CL_W10_WebAPI::begin(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    // API 인증 (v0203은 X-API-Key 헤더)
    // (개발 중엔 빈 키 허용)

    registerSessionRoutes(server);
    registerProfileRoutes(server);
    registerScheduleRoutes(server);
    registerStateRoutes(server);
    registerWsRoutes(server);
    registerStaticRoutes(server);

    CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO, "[W10] All routes registered");
}
```

2.3 W10_Web_Util_0203.cpp — 공용 유틸

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"
#include "D10_Logger_0203.h"

bool W10_checkApiKey(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!g_A20_config_root.system) return true;   // config 미로드 시 개방
    const char* expected = g_A20_config_root.system->security.apiKey;
    if (!expected || !expected[0]) return true;   // 빈 키 = 개방

    if (!req->hasHeader("X-API-Key")) return false;
    return strcmp(req->header("X-API-Key").c_str(), expected) == 0;
}

bool W10_parseJsonBody(AsyncWebServerRequest* req, JsonDocument& doc,
                       uint8_t* buf, size_t bufSize) {
    // AsyncWebServer의 body는 콜백으로만 접근 가능
    // v0203은 간단히 POST 쿼리 파라미터 또는 raw body 사용
    if (req->hasParam("plain", true)) {
        auto err = deserializeJson(doc, req->getParam("plain", true)->value());
        return !err;
    }
    return false;
}

void W10_sendJson(AsyncWebServerRequest* req, int code, const JsonDocument& doc) {
    String out;
    serializeJson(doc, out);
    req->send(code, "application/json", out);
}
```

2.4 W10_Web_Routes_Session_0203.cpp — Session API ★

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// POST /api/v0203/session/start
// Body: { "type": "PLAYLIST"|"PRESET"|"FIXED", "payload": {...} }
// ======================================================
static void handleSessionStart(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) {
        req->send(401, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"unauthorized\"}");
        return;
    }

    JsonDocument in;
    uint8_t buf[1024];
    if (!W10_parseJsonBody(req, in, buf, sizeof(buf))) {
        req->send(400, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"invalid_body\"}");
        return;
    }

    const char* typeStr = A40_ComFunc::Json_getStr(in.as<JsonObjectConst>(), "type", "");
    EN_CT10_session_type_t type = EN_CT10_SESSION_NONE;
    if (strcmp(typeStr, "PLAYLIST") == 0)  type = EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST;
    else if (strcmp(typeStr, "PRESET") == 0)  type = EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET;
    else if (strcmp(typeStr, "FIXED") == 0)   type = EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED;
    else {
        req->send(400, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"invalid_type\"}");
        return;
    }

    JsonObjectConst payload = in["payload"].as<JsonObjectConst>();
    JsonDocument pDoc;
    pDoc.set(payload);

    bool ok = CL_CT10_ControlManager::startSession(type,
                                                   EN_CT10_TRIGGER_MANUAL, pDoc);

    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    if (!ok) out["error"] = "start_failed";
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 400, out);
}

// ======================================================
// POST /api/v0203/session/pause
// ======================================================
static void handleSessionPause(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    bool ok = CL_CT10_ControlManager::pauseSession();
    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    if (!ok) out["error"] = "not_active";
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 400, out);
}

// ======================================================
// POST /api/v0203/session/resume
// ======================================================
static void handleSessionResume(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    bool ok = CL_CT10_ControlManager::resumeSession();
    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    if (!ok) out["error"] = "not_paused";
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 400, out);
}

// ======================================================
// POST /api/v0203/session/stop
// ======================================================
static void handleSessionStop(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    bool ok = CL_CT10_ControlManager::stopSession();
    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    W10_sendJson(req, 200, out);
}

// ======================================================
// GET /api/v0203/session/current
// ======================================================
static void handleSessionCurrent(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    auto s = CL_CT10_ControlManager::getSessionSnapshot();
    JsonDocument out;

    out["type"]    = (uint8_t)s.type;
    out["trigger"] = (uint8_t)s.trigger;
    out["state"]   = (uint8_t)s.state;

    if (s.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PLAYLIST) {
        out["profileId"]   = s.payload.playlist.profileId;
        out["profileNo"]   = s.payload.playlist.profileNo;
        out["currentStep"] = s.payload.playlist.currentStepIdx + 1;
        out["totalSteps"]  = s.payload.playlist.totalSteps;
        out["loopCount"]   = s.payload.playlist.loopCount;
    } else if (s.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_PRESET) {
        out["presetCode"] = s.payload.preset.presetCode;
        out["styleCode"]  = s.payload.preset.styleCode;
    } else if (s.type == EN_CT10_SESSION_FIXED) {
        out["percent"] = s.payload.fixed.percent;
    }

    out["pausedAccumMs"] = s.pausedAccumMs;
    W10_sendJson(req, 200, out);
}

// ======================================================
// 등록
// ======================================================
void CL_W10_WebAPI::registerSessionRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    server.on("/api/v0203/session/start",  HTTP_POST, handleSessionStart);
    server.on("/api/v0203/session/pause",  HTTP_POST, handleSessionPause);
    server.on("/api/v0203/session/resume", HTTP_POST, handleSessionResume);
    server.on("/api/v0203/session/stop",   HTTP_POST, handleSessionStop);
    server.on("/api/v0203/session/current", HTTP_GET, handleSessionCurrent);
}
```

2.5 W10_Web_Routes_Profile_0203.cpp — Profile API

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"

// ======================================================
// GET /api/v0203/profiles
// ======================================================
static void handleProfileList(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    ST_A20_ConfigRoot_t snap;
    CL_C10_ConfigManager::getRootSnapshot(snap);

    JsonDocument out;
    if (snap.profiles) {
        CL_C10_ConfigManager::toJson_Profiles(*snap.profiles, out);
    }
    W10_sendJson(req, 200, out);
}

// ======================================================
// POST /api/v0203/profiles
// Body: { "profile": {...} }
// ======================================================
static void handleProfileAdd(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    JsonDocument in;
    uint8_t buf[2048];
    if (!W10_parseJsonBody(req, in, buf, sizeof(buf))) {
        req->send(400, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"invalid_body\"}");
        return;
    }

    int idx = CL_C10_ConfigManager::addProfileFromJson(in);
    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = (idx >= 0);
    out["index"] = idx;

    if (idx >= 0) {
        CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveProfiles(*g_A20_config_root.profiles);
    }
    W10_sendJson(req, idx >= 0 ? 200 : 400, out);
}

// ======================================================
// PUT /api/v0203/profiles/:id
// ======================================================
static void handleProfileUpdate(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    if (!req->hasParam("id")) {
        req->send(400, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"missing_id\"}");
        return;
    }
    uint16_t id = (uint16_t)req->getParam("id")->value().toInt();

    JsonDocument in;
    uint8_t buf[2048];
    if (!W10_parseJsonBody(req, in, buf, sizeof(buf))) {
        req->send(400); return;
    }

    bool ok = CL_C10_ConfigManager::updateProfileFromJson(id, in);
    if (ok) CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveProfiles(*g_A20_config_root.profiles);

    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 400, out);
}

// ======================================================
// DELETE /api/v0203/profiles/:id
// ======================================================
static void handleProfileDelete(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    if (!req->hasParam("id")) { req->send(400); return; }
    uint16_t id = (uint16_t)req->getParam("id")->value().toInt();

    bool ok = CL_C10_ConfigManager::deleteProfile(id);
    if (ok) {
        CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveProfiles(*g_A20_config_root.profiles);
        CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveSchedules(*g_A20_config_root.schedules);
    }

    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 404, out);
}

// ======================================================
// 등록 — onNotFound로 /:id 처리
// ======================================================
void CL_W10_WebAPI::registerProfileRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    server.on("/api/v0203/profiles", HTTP_GET,  handleProfileList);
    server.on("/api/v0203/profiles", HTTP_POST, handleProfileAdd);

    // /:id 처리 (v0203은 onNotFound 활용)
    server.on("/api/v0203/profiles/", HTTP_ANY,
        [](AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
            String url = req->url();
            int lastSlash = url.lastIndexOf('/');
            if (lastSlash < 0) { req->send(400); return; }
            String idStr = url.substring(lastSlash + 1);

            // req->addParam 미지원 → 임시로 url 파싱
            // (간단 구현: v0203은 Stage 3에서 이렇게 처리)
            if (req->method() == HTTP_PUT) {
                // Fake id param
                // ⚠️ Stage 4에서 정교화 예정
                req->send(501, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"use_body_id\"}");
                return;
            }
            if (req->method() == HTTP_DELETE) {
                // 임시: body로 id 전달
                req->send(501, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"use_body_id\"}");
                return;
            }
            req->send(404);
        });
}
```

⚠️ 중요 결정 — :id 라우팅 우회

문제: AsyncWebServer의 정규식 라우팅은 platformio.ini의 -D ASYNCWEBSERVER_REGEX 플래그가 필요하며, 이 플래그는 v0202에서 제거된 상태.

Stage 3 임시 결정:

· Stage 3은 body에 id를 포함하는 방식으로 처리:
  ```json
  PUT /api/v0203/profiles
  Body: { "profileId": 1, "profile": {...} }
  ```
· Stage 4에서 ASYNCWEBSERVER_REGEX 재도입 또는 명시적 path 파싱으로 정교화
· 이유: Stage 3 목표는 "웹에서 최초 조작"이므로 API 완벽성보다 동작 검증 우선

수정된 최종 API 스펙:

Method Endpoint Body
GET /api/v0203/profiles -
POST /api/v0203/profiles {profile}
PUT /api/v0203/profiles/update {profileId, profile}
DELETE /api/v0203/profiles/delete {profileId}

2.6 W10_Web_Routes_Schedule_0203.cpp — Schedule API

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "C10_Config_0203.h"

static void handleScheduleList(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    ST_A20_ConfigRoot_t snap;
    CL_C10_ConfigManager::getRootSnapshot(snap);

    JsonDocument out;
    if (snap.schedules) {
        CL_C10_ConfigManager::toJson_Schedules(*snap.schedules, out);
    }
    W10_sendJson(req, 200, out);
}

static void handleScheduleAdd(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    JsonDocument in;
    uint8_t buf[2048];
    if (!W10_parseJsonBody(req, in, buf, sizeof(buf))) {
        req->send(400); return;
    }

    int idx = CL_C10_ConfigManager::addScheduleFromJson(in);
    if (idx >= 0) CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveSchedules(*g_A20_config_root.schedules);

    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = (idx >= 0);
    out["index"] = idx;
    W10_sendJson(req, idx >= 0 ? 200 : 400, out);
}

static void handleScheduleUpdate(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    JsonDocument in;
    uint8_t buf[2048];
    if (!W10_parseJsonBody(req, in, buf, sizeof(buf))) {
        req->send(400); return;
    }

    uint16_t schId = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(
        in.as<JsonObjectConst>(), "schId", 0);

    bool ok = CL_C10_ConfigManager::updateScheduleFromJson(schId, in);
    if (ok) CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveSchedules(*g_A20_config_root.schedules);

    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 400, out);
}

static void handleScheduleDelete(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    JsonDocument in;
    uint8_t buf[512];
    if (!W10_parseJsonBody(req, in, buf, sizeof(buf))) {
        req->send(400); return;
    }

    uint16_t schId = A40_ComFunc::Json_getNum<uint16_t>(
        in.as<JsonObjectConst>(), "schId", 0);

    bool ok = CL_C10_ConfigManager::deleteSchedule(schId);
    if (ok) CL_C10_ConfigManager::saveSchedules(*g_A20_config_root.schedules);

    JsonDocument out;
    out["ok"] = ok;
    W10_sendJson(req, ok ? 200 : 404, out);
}

void CL_W10_WebAPI::registerScheduleRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    server.on("/api/v0203/schedules",        HTTP_GET,  handleScheduleList);
    server.on("/api/v0203/schedules",        HTTP_POST, handleScheduleAdd);
    server.on("/api/v0203/schedules/update", HTTP_POST, handleScheduleUpdate);
    server.on("/api/v0203/schedules/delete", HTTP_POST, handleScheduleDelete);
}
```

#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"

static void handleState(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!W10_checkApiKey(req)) { req->send(401); return; }

    JsonDocument out;
    CL_CT10_ControlManager::instance().exportStateJson_v02(out);
    W10_sendJson(req, 200, out);
}

static void handleVersion(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    JsonDocument out;
    out["fw"]      = A20_Const::FW_VERSION;
    out["control"] = A20_Const::VER_CONTROL;
    out["config"]  = A20_Const::VER_CONFIG;
    out["api"]     = A20_Const::VER_API;
    out["simul"]   = A20_Const::VER_SIMUL;
    W10_sendJson(req, 200, out);
}

void CL_W10_WebAPI::registerStateRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    server.on("/api/v0203/state",   HTTP_GET, handleState);
    server.on("/api/v0203/version", HTTP_GET, handleVersion);
}

2.8 W10_Web_WS_0203.cpp — WebSocket (최소)

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include "CT10_Ctl_0203.h"
#include <ESPAsyncWebServer.h>

static AsyncWebSocket s_wsState("/ws/state");
static uint32_t s_lastPushMs = 0;

static void onWsEvent(AsyncWebSocket* server, AsyncWebSocketClient* client,
                      AwsEventType type, void* arg, uint8_t* data, size_t len) {
    if (type == WS_EVT_CONNECT) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[WS] client connected id=%u", client->id());
    } else if (type == WS_EVT_DISCONNECT) {
        CL_D10_Logger::log(EN_L10_LOG_INFO,
            "[WS] client disconnected id=%u", client->id());
    }
}

static void wsTick() {
    if (s_wsState.count() == 0) return;
    uint32_t now = millis();
    if (now - s_lastPushMs < 800) return;   // 800ms 주기
    s_lastPushMs = now;

    JsonDocument doc;
    CL_CT10_ControlManager::instance().exportStateJson_v02(doc);
    String out;
    serializeJson(doc, out);
    s_wsState.textAll(out);
}

// A00_run에서 호출
void CT10_WS_tick_0203() { wsTick(); }

void CL_W10_WebAPI::registerWsRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    s_wsState.onEvent(onWsEvent);
    server.addHandler(&s_wsState);
}
```

2.9 W10_Web_Static_0203.cpp — 정적 서빙 (최소)

```cpp
#include "W10_Web_0203.h"
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include "A40_Com_Utils_0203.h"

static const char* PATH_STATIC = "/html_v0203";

static String guessMime(const String& path) {
    if (path.endsWith(".html")) return "text/html";
    if (path.endsWith(".css"))  return "text/css";
    if (path.endsWith(".js"))   return "application/javascript";
    if (path.endsWith(".json")) return "application/json";
    if (path.endsWith(".ico"))  return "image/x-icon";
    if (path.endsWith(".svg"))  return "image/svg+xml";
    return "application/octet-stream";
}

static bool acceptsGzip(AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
    if (!req->hasHeader("Accept-Encoding")) return false;
    String ae = req->header("Accept-Encoding");
    return ae.indexOf("gzip") >= 0;
}

void CL_W10_WebAPI::registerStaticRoutes(AsyncWebServer& server) {
    // 루트 → /index.html 리다이렉트
    server.on("/", HTTP_GET, [](AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
        req->redirect("/index.html");
    });

    // onNotFound로 정적 서빙
    server.onNotFound([](AsyncWebServerRequest* req) {
        String url = req->url();
        if (!url.startsWith("/")) url = "/" + url;

        // /html_v0203 접두사 정규화
        String fsPath = String(PATH_STATIC) + url;
        if (url.startsWith(PATH_STATIC)) fsPath = url;

        String gzPath = fsPath + ".gz";

        // gzip 우선
        if (acceptsGzip(req) && LittleFS.exists(gzPath)) {
            AsyncWebServerResponse* res = req->beginResponse(
                LittleFS, gzPath, guessMime(fsPath));
            res->addHeader("Content-Encoding", "gzip");
            res->addHeader("Vary", "Accept-Encoding");
            req->send(res);
            return;
        }

        // raw
        if (LittleFS.exists(fsPath)) {
            AsyncWebServerResponse* res = req->beginResponse(
                LittleFS, fsPath, guessMime(fsPath));
            res->addHeader("Vary", "Accept-Encoding");
            req->send(res);
            return;
        }

        // 진단 404
        req->send(404, "text/plain",
            String("Not found: ") + url + "\n"
            "Try: /index.html");
    });
}
```

3.4 040.api.js — REST 클라이언트

```javascript
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    function getApiKey() {
        try { return localStorage.getItem(SNW.CFG.LS_KEY.API_KEY) || ''; }
        catch { return ''; }
    }

    async function _fetch(path, opts = {}) {
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timeoutMs = opts.timeoutMs || SNW.CFG.TIMEOUT_MS;
        const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

        try {
            const res = await fetch(path, {
                method: opts.method || 'GET',
                headers: {
                    'X-API-Key': getApiKey(),
                    ...(opts.body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
                    ...(opts.headers || {}),
                },
                body: opts.body ? JSON.stringify(opts.body) : undefined,
                signal: controller.signal,
            });
            clearTimeout(timer);

            const ct = res.headers.get('Content-Type') || '';
            const data = ct.includes('json') ? await res.json() : await res.text();

            if (!res.ok) {
                const err = new Error((data && data.error) || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
                err.status = res.status;
                throw err;
            }
            return data;
        } catch (e) {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            throw e;
        }
    }

    SNW.Api = {
        // Version / State
        getVersion: () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.version),
        getState:   () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.state),

        // Session
        startPlaylist: (profileId) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionStart, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { type: 'PLAYLIST', payload: { profileId } },
            }),
        startPreset: (presetCode, styleCode) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionStart, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { type: 'PRESET', payload: { presetCode, styleCode } },
            }),
        startFixed: (percent) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionStart, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { type: 'FIXED', payload: { percent } },
            }),
        pauseSession:  () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionPause,  { method: 'POST' }),
        resumeSession: () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionResume, { method: 'POST' }),
        stopSession:   () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionStop,   { method: 'POST' }),
        getCurrentSession: () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.sessionCurrent),

        // Profiles
        listProfiles:  () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.profiles),
        addProfile:    (profile) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.profiles, { method: 'POST', body: { profile } }),
        updateProfile: (profileId, profile) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.profileUpdate, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { profileId, profile },
            }),
        deleteProfile: (profileId) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.profileDelete, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { profileId },
            }),

        // Schedules
        listSchedules:  () => _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.schedules),
        addSchedule:    (schedule) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.schedules, { method: 'POST', body: { schedule } }),
        updateSchedule: (schId, schedule) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.scheduleUpdate, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { schId, schedule },
            }),
        deleteSchedule: (schId) =>
            _fetch(SNW.CFG.API.scheduleDelete, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: { schId },
            }),
    };
})(window.SNW);
```

3.5 050.ws.js — WebSocket 클라이언트

```javascript
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    class WsClient {
        constructor(channel) {
            this.channel = channel;
            this.ws = null;
            this.subs = new Set();
            this.openHandlers = new Set();
            this.closeHandlers = new Set();
            this.attempt = 0;
            this.closed = false;
        }

        url() {
            const key = localStorage.getItem(SNW.CFG.LS_KEY.API_KEY) || '';
            const q = key ? `?apiKey=${encodeURIComponent(key)}` : '';
            const proto = (location.protocol === 'https:') ? 'wss' : 'ws';
            return `${proto}://${location.host}${SNW.CFG.WS_PATH[this.channel]}${q}`;
        }

        connect() {
            if (this.closed) return;
            try { this.ws = new WebSocket(this.url()); }
            catch { return this._scheduleReconnect(); }

            this.ws.onopen = () => {
                this.attempt = 0;
                this.openHandlers.forEach(h => { try { h(); } catch {} });
            };
            this.ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
                let data; try { data = JSON.parse(ev.data); } catch { return; }
                this.subs.forEach(fn => { try { fn(data); } catch {} });
            };
            this.ws.onerror = () => {};
            this.ws.onclose = () => {
                if (this.closed) return;
                this.closeHandlers.forEach(h => { try { h(); } catch {} });
                this._scheduleReconnect();
            };
        }

        _scheduleReconnect() {
            if (this.closed) return;
            const backoff = [1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 16000, 30000];
            const wait = backoff[Math.min(this.attempt, backoff.length - 1)];
            this.attempt++;
            setTimeout(() => this.connect(), wait);
        }

        onMessage(fn) { this.subs.add(fn); return () => this.subs.delete(fn); }
        onOpen(fn)    { this.openHandlers.add(fn); return () => this.openHandlers.delete(fn); }
        onClose(fn)   { this.closeHandlers.add(fn); return () => this.closeHandlers.delete(fn); }

        close() {
            this.closed = true;
            if (this.ws) { try { this.ws.close(); } catch {} }
        }
    }

    SNW.WsManager = {
        _clients: {},
        get(channel) {
            if (!this._clients[channel]) {
                const c = new WsClient(channel);
                this._clients[channel] = c;
                c.connect();
            }
            return this._clients[channel];
        },
        closeAll() {
            Object.values(this._clients).forEach(c => c.close());
            this._clients = {};
        },
    };
})(window.SNW);
```

3.6 060.store.js — Store

```javascript
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    class Store {
        constructor(initial) {
            this._state = Object.assign({}, initial);
            this._subs = new Set();
        }
        get() { return this._state; }
        subscribe(fn) { this._subs.add(fn); return () => this._subs.delete(fn); }
        set(patch) {
            const prev = this._state;
            this._state = Object.assign({}, prev, patch);
            this._subs.forEach(fn => { try { fn(this._state, prev); } catch {} });
        }
        watch(key, fn) {
            let last = this._state[key];
            return this.subscribe((s) => {
                if (s[key] !== last) {
                    const prev = last;
                    last = s[key];
                    fn(s[key], prev);
                }
            });
        }
    }

    SNW.Store = new Store({
        control: null,
        sim: null,
        lastUpdateMs: 0,
        connection: 'offline',
        session: null,         // ★ Session 스냅샷
    });

    SNW.StoreBridge = {
        _started: false,
        _unsubs: [],
        start() {
            if (this._started) return;
            this._started = true;

            const ws = SNW.WsManager.get('state');
            if (!ws) return;

            const unsub = ws.onMessage((data) => {
                const ctl = data.control || null;
                const sim = data.sim || null;
                const session = (ctl && ctl.session) || null;

                SNW.Store.set({
                    control: ctl,
                    sim,
                    session,
                    connection: 'online',
                    lastUpdateMs: Date.now(),
                });
            });

            ws.onClose(() => SNW.Store.set({ connection: 'offline' }));
            ws.onOpen(() => SNW.Store.set({ connection: 'online' }));

            this._unsubs.push(unsub);
        },
    };
})(window.SNW);
```

3.7 070.router.js — 라우터

```javascript
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    const ROUTES = {
        '/dashboard':  { page: 'dashboard',  title: '홈' },
        '/wind':       { page: 'wind',       title: '바람' },
        '/automation': { page: 'automation', title: '자동화' },
        '/monitor':    { page: 'monitor',    title: '모니터' },
        '/settings':   { page: 'settings',   title: '설정' },
    };

    let currentPage = null;
    let currentPath = null;
    let dispatching = false;

    function parseHash() {
        const raw = location.hash.replace(/^#/, '') || '/dashboard';
        return ROUTES[raw] ? raw : '/dashboard';
    }

    function clearMain() {
        const main = document.getElementById('snw-main');
        if (main) main.innerHTML = '';
        return main;
    }

    async function dispatch() {
        if (dispatching) return;
        const path = parseHash();
        if (path === currentPath) return;

        dispatching = true;
        try {
            if (currentPage && typeof currentPage.unmount === 'function') {
                currentPage.unmount();
            }
            currentPage = null;
            currentPath = null;

            const main = clearMain();
            const PageClass = SNW.Page && SNW.Page[capitalize(ROUTES[path].page)];
            if (!PageClass) {
                main.innerHTML = `<div class="snw-error">Page not implemented: ${path}</div>`;
                return;
            }

            currentPage = new PageClass(main);
            currentPath = path;
            await currentPage.mount();

            // 네비 active
            document.querySelectorAll('.snw-bottom-nav__item').forEach(a => {
                a.classList.toggle('is-active', a.getAttribute('data-route') === path);
            });
        } finally {
            dispatching = false;
        }
    }

    function capitalize(s) {
        return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
    }

    SNW.Page = SNW.Page || {};

    SNW.Router = {
        init() {
            window.addEventListener('hashchange', dispatch);
            dispatch();
        },
        navigate(path) { location.hash = path; },
        current() { return currentPath; },
    };
})(window.SNW);
```

3.8 999a.nowplaying.js — Now Playing Bar ★핵심

```javascript
// =========================================================
// 999a.nowplaying.js — 전역 Now Playing Bar
// =========================================================
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    const barEl = {
        root: null,
        eq: null,
        title: null,
        step: null,
        time: null,
        pending: null,
        btnPause: null,
        btnStop: null,
        // 로컬 상태
        _lastSession: null,
        _eqAnimId: null,
        _eqBuffer: [],
    };

    function $(sel, root) { return (root || document).querySelector(sel); }

    function getSession() {
        return SNW.Store.get().session;
    }

    function isSessionVisible() {
        const s = getSession();
        if (!s) return false;
        const type = (typeof s.type === 'string') ? parseType(s.type) : s.type;
        return type !== 0 && type !== 'NONE';
    }

    function parseType(t) {
        const m = { NONE: 0, PLAYLIST: 1, PRESET: 2, FIXED: 3 };
        return m[t] != null ? m[t] : 0;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 렌더링
    // =========================================================
    function render() {
        if (!barEl.root) return;

        const s = getSession();
        if (!s || !isSessionVisible()) {
            barEl.root.hidden = true;
            stopEq();
            return;
        }
        barEl.root.hidden = false;

        const type    = parseType(s.type);
        const trigger = (typeof s.trigger === 'string') ? s.trigger : '';
        const state   = (typeof s.state === 'string') ? s.state : '';
        const isPaused = state === 'PAUSED';

        // 타이틀
        let title = '';
        if (type === 1)      title = s.profileName || '플레이리스트';
        else if (type === 2) title = `${s.presetCode || '-'} · ${s.styleCode || '-'}`;
        else if (type === 3) title = `일반 선풍기 (${Math.round(s.percent || 0)}%)`;
        barEl.title.textContent = title;

        // 서브
        if (type === 1) {
            barEl.step.textContent = `Step ${s.currentStep || 0}/${s.totalSteps || 0}`;
            const remainSec = s.stepRemainSec || 0;
            barEl.time.textContent = isPaused
                ? `일시정지 (${formatTime(remainSec)})`
                : formatTime(remainSec);
        } else if (type === 3) {
            barEl.step.textContent = `${Math.round(s.percent || 0)}%`;
            barEl.time.textContent = isPaused ? '일시정지' : '';
        } else {
            barEl.step.textContent = '';
            barEl.time.textContent = isPaused ? '일시정지' : '';
        }

        // Pause/Play 버튼
        barEl.btnPause.textContent = isPaused ? '▶' : '⏸';
        barEl.btnPause.setAttribute('aria-label', isPaused ? '재개' : '일시정지');

        // Pending Schedule
        const ctl = SNW.Store.get().control;
        const ps = ctl && ctl.pendingSchedule;
        if (ps && ps.active && ps.inactiveDueToManual) {
            barEl.pending.hidden = false;
            barEl.pending.innerHTML = `<span class="strike">⏰ ${ps.startTime || ''}</span>`;
        } else {
            barEl.pending.hidden = true;
        }

        // EQ 시작/중지
        if (isPaused) {
            stopEq();
            renderEqStatic();
        } else {
            startEq();
        }
    }

    function formatTime(sec) {
        if (!sec || sec < 0) return '0:00';
        const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
        const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
        return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 이퀄라이저 (간단 파형)
    // =========================================================
    function startEq() {
        if (barEl._eqAnimId) return;
        const ctx = barEl.eq.getContext('2d');
        const w = barEl.eq.width;
        const h = barEl.eq.height;

        const tick = () => {
            // sim의 최근 풍속을 반영 (없으면 랜덤)
            const sim = SNW.Store.get().sim;
            const v = (sim && sim.windSpeed) || 0;
            barEl._eqBuffer.push(v);
            if (barEl._eqBuffer.length > 6) barEl._eqBuffer.shift();

            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
            const bars = barEl._eqBuffer.length;
            const bw = w / Math.max(bars, 1);
            for (let i = 0; i < bars; i++) {
                const val = barEl._eqBuffer[i] || 0;
                const bh = Math.max(2, (val / 11) * h);
                ctx.fillStyle = val > 6 ? '#f0883e' : val > 3 ? '#3fb950' : '#4a9eff';
                ctx.fillRect(i * bw, h - bh, bw - 1, bh);
            }
            barEl._eqAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };
        tick();
    }

    function stopEq() {
        if (barEl._eqAnimId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(barEl._eqAnimId);
            barEl._eqAnimId = null;
        }
    }

    function renderEqStatic() {
        const ctx = barEl.eq.getContext('2d');
        const w = barEl.eq.width, h = barEl.eq.height;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#59636e';
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            ctx.fillRect(i * (w / 4) + 1, h / 2, w / 4 - 2, 2);
        }
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 액션
    // =========================================================
    async function onPauseOrResume() {
        const s = getSession();
        if (!s) return;
        try {
            if (s.state === 'PAUSED') {
                await SNW.Api.resumeSession();
                SNW.ToastUtil.success('재개');
            } else {
                await SNW.Api.pauseSession();
                SNW.ToastUtil.info('일시정지');
            }
        } catch (e) {
            SNW.ToastUtil.error(e.message || '실패');
        }
    }

    async function onStop() {
        try {
            await SNW.Api.stopSession();
            SNW.ToastUtil.info('정지');
        } catch (e) {
            SNW.ToastUtil.error(e.message || '실패');
        }
    }

    function onExpand() {
        // 현재 Session 타입에 따라 상세 이동
        const s = getSession();
        if (!s) return;
        const type = parseType(s.type);
        if (type === 1)      location.hash = '/automation';
        else if (type === 2) location.hash = '/wind';
        else if (type === 3) location.hash = '/dashboard';
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 초기화
    // =========================================================
    function init() {
        barEl.root = document.getElementById('now-playing-bar');
        if (!barEl.root) return;

        barEl.eq      = $('.snw-nowplaying__eq', barEl.root);
        barEl.title   = $('.snw-nowplaying__title', barEl.root);
        barEl.step    = $('[data-slot="np-step"]', barEl.root);
        barEl.time    = $('[data-slot="np-time"]', barEl.root);
        barEl.pending = $('[data-slot="np-pending"]', barEl.root);
        barEl.btnPause = $('[data-action="pause"]', barEl.root);
        barEl.btnStop  = $('[data-action="stop"]', barEl.root);

        barEl.btnPause.addEventListener('click', onPauseOrResume);
        barEl.btnStop.addEventListener('click', onStop);
        $('[data-action="expand"]', barEl.root).addEventListener('click', onExpand);

        // Store 구독
        SNW.Store.subscribe(() => render());
        render();
    }

    SNW.NowPlaying = { init };
})(window.SNW);
```

3.9 200.page.dashboard.js — 최소 홈

```javascript
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
SNW.Page = SNW.Page || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    SNW.Page.Dashboard = class Dashboard {
        constructor(container) {
            this.el = container;
            this._unsubs = [];
            this._active = true;
        }

        async mount() {
            this.el.innerHTML = '';
            this._render();
            this._bindStore();
            await this._loadProfiles();
        }

        unmount() {
            this._active = false;
            this._unsubs.forEach(fn => { try { fn(); } catch {} });
            this._unsubs = [];
        }

        _render() {
            this.el.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-dashboard">
                    <!-- Now Playing Card -->
                    <section class="snw-card snw-card--nowplaying">
                        <h2>지금 재생 중</h2>
                        <div class="snw-np-card" data-slot="np-card">
                            <div class="snw-np-card__empty">
                                현재 재생 중인 세션이 없습니다
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </section>

                    <!-- 즐겨찾기 프로필 (원터치 재생) -->
                    <section class="snw-card snw-card--playlist">
                        <h2>🎵 재생할 플레이리스트</h2>
                        <div data-slot="playlist-grid" class="snw-playlist-grid">
                            <div class="snw-empty">로딩 중...</div>
                        </div>
                    </section>

                    <!-- 일반 선풍기 (최하단, 엄지존) -->
                    <section class="snw-card snw-card--fan">
                        <h2>🌀 일반 선풍기</h2>
                        <div class="snw-fan-steps">
                            <button class="snw-fan-step" data-percent="0">끔</button>
                            <button class="snw-fan-step" data-percent="20">1단</button>
                            <button class="snw-fan-step" data-percent="40">2단</button>
                            <button class="snw-fan-step" data-percent="60">3단</button>
                            <button class="snw-fan-step" data-percent="80">4단</button>
                            <button class="snw-fan-step" data-percent="100">5단</button>
                        </div>
                    </section>

                    <!-- 센서 요약 -->
                    <section class="snw-card snw-card--sensor">
                        <div data-slot="sensor-summary" class="snw-sensor-summary">
                            🌡️ --°C  💧 --%  👤 --
                        </div>
                    </section>
                </div>`;

            // 일반 선풍기 버튼 바인딩
            this.el.querySelectorAll('.snw-fan-step').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => this._onFanStep(
                    Number(btn.getAttribute('data-percent'))));
            });
        }

        _bindStore() {
            this._unsubs.push(
                SNW.Store.watch('session', () => this._renderNowPlaying()),
                SNW.Store.watch('control', () => this._renderSensorSummary()),
            );
        }

        async _loadProfiles() {
            try {
                const data = await SNW.Api.listProfiles();
                const profiles = (data && data.profiles) || [];
                const grid = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="playlist-grid"]');

                if (profiles.length === 0) {
                    grid.innerHTML = `<div class="snw-empty">아직 등록된 프로필이 없습니다.</div>`;
                    return;
                }

                grid.innerHTML = '';
                profiles.slice(0, 4).forEach(p => {
                    const card = document.createElement('button');
                    card.className = 'snw-playlist-card';
                    card.setAttribute('data-profile-id', p.profileId);
                    card.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="snw-playlist-card__icon">🌊</div>
                        <div class="snw-playlist-card__name">${SNW.DomUtil.escape(p.name || '프로필')}</div>
                        <div class="snw-playlist-card__meta">${p.stepCount || 0}개 Step</div>`;
                    card.addEventListener('click', () => this._onPlayProfile(p.profileId));
                    grid.appendChild(card);
                });
            } catch (e) {
                SNW.LogUtil.warn('[Dashboard] profiles load fail', e);
            }
        }

        _renderNowPlaying() {
            const s = SNW.Store.get().session;
            const slot = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="np-card"]');
            if (!slot) return;

            if (!s || !s.type || s.type === 'NONE') {
                slot.innerHTML = `<div class="snw-np-card__empty">현재 재생 중인 세션이 없습니다</div>`;
                return;
            }

            const type = s.type;
            const isPaused = s.state === 'PAUSED';
            let title = '';
            let sub = '';

            if (type === 'PLAYLIST') {
                title = s.profileName || '플레이리스트';
                sub = `Step ${s.currentStep}/${s.totalSteps} · ${this._fmtRemain(s.stepRemainSec)}`;
            } else if (type === 'PRESET') {
                title = `${s.presetCode} · ${s.styleCode}`;
                sub = isPaused ? '일시정지' : '재생 중';
            } else if (type === 'FIXED') {
                title = `일반 선풍기 ${Math.round(s.percent || 0)}%`;
                sub = isPaused ? '일시정지' : '재생 중';
            }

            const triggerIcon = s.trigger === 'MANUAL' ? '✋' : '⏰';
            slot.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-np-card__main">
                    <div class="snw-np-card__title">
                        ${triggerIcon} ${SNW.DomUtil.escape(title)}
                        ${isPaused ? '<span class="snw-np-card__paused">일시정지</span>' : ''}
                    </div>
                    <div class="snw-np-card__sub">${SNW.DomUtil.escape(sub)}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="snw-np-card__controls">
                    <button class="snw-btn" data-action="pause">
                        ${isPaused ? '▶ 재개' : '⏸ 일시정지'}
                    </button>
                    <button class="snw-btn" data-action="stop">⏹ 정지</button>
                </div>`;

            slot.querySelector('[data-action="pause"]').onclick = async () => {
                try {
                    if (isPaused) await SNW.Api.resumeSession();
                    else           await SNW.Api.pauseSession();
                } catch (e) { SNW.ToastUtil.error(e.message); }
            };
            slot.querySelector('[data-action="stop"]').onclick = async () => {
                try { await SNW.Api.stopSession(); }
                catch (e) { SNW.ToastUtil.error(e.message); }
            };
        }

        _renderSensorSummary() {
            const ctl = SNW.Store.get().control;
            const slot = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="sensor-summary"]');
            if (!slot || !ctl || !ctl.sensor) return;
            const sens = ctl.sensor;
            slot.innerHTML = `
                🌡️ ${sens.tempC != null ? sens.tempC.toFixed(1) : '--'}°C
                💧 ${sens.humidity != null ? sens.humidity.toFixed(0) : '--'}%
                👤 ${sens.motionActive ? '재실' : '부재'}`;
        }

        async _onFanStep(percent) {
            try {
                if (percent === 0) {
                    await SNW.Api.stopSession();
                } else {
                    await SNW.Api.startFixed(percent);
                }
                // 햅틱 (지원 시)
                if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(15);
            } catch (e) {
                SNW.ToastUtil.error(e.message);
            }
        }

        async _onPlayProfile(profileId) {
            try {
                await SNW.Api.startPlaylist(profileId);
                if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([10, 20, 10]);
            } catch (e) {
                SNW.ToastUtil.error(e.message);
            }
        }

        _fmtRemain(sec) {
            if (!sec || sec < 0) return '0:00';
            const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
            const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
            return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        }
    };

    // 나머지 4 페이지는 Stage 3에서 최소 stub
    SNW.Page.Wind       = class { async mount() { this.el = this.el; } };
    SNW.Page.Automation = class { constructor(el) { this.el = el; } async mount() { this.el.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">Stage 4에서 구현</div>'; } };
    SNW.Page.Monitor    = class { constructor(el) { this.el = el; } async mount() { this.el.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">Stage 6에서 구현</div>'; } };
    SNW.Page.Settings   = class { constructor(el) { this.el = el; } async mount() { this.el.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">Stage 7에서 구현</div>'; } };
})(window.SNW);
```

    async mount() { this.el.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">Stage 4에서 구현</div>'; }

3.10 999.app.js — 부트스트랩

```javascript
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    // =========================================================
    // Toast (최소)
    // =========================================================
    SNW.ToastUtil = {
        _host: null,
        _ensure() {
            if (this._host) return this._host;
            this._host = document.getElementById('toast-host');
            return this._host;
        },
        show(msg, type, ms) {
            const host = this._ensure();
            if (!host) return;
            const t = document.createElement('div');
            t.className = `snw-toast snw-toast--${type || 'info'}`;
            t.textContent = String(msg);
            host.appendChild(t);
            setTimeout(() => t.classList.add('snw-toast--out'), (ms || 3000) - 300);
            setTimeout(() => t.remove(), ms || 3000);
        },
        info(m, t)    { this.show(m, 'info', t); },
        success(m, t) { this.show(m, 'success', t || 2000); },
        warn(m, t)    { this.show(m, 'warn', t); },
        error(m, t)   { this.show(m, 'error', t || 4000); },
    };

    SNW.LogUtil = {
        debug(...a) { console.debug('[SNW]', ...a); },
        info(...a)  { console.info('[SNW]', ...a); },
        warn(...a)  { console.warn('[SNW]', ...a); },
        error(...a) { console.error('[SNW]', ...a); },
    };

    SNW.DomUtil = {
        escape(s) {
            return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
                '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;',
                '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;',
            }[c]));
        },
    };

    // =========================================================
    // 사이드바 토글
    // =========================================================
    function bindSidebar() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-menu');
        const sidebar = document.getElementById('snw-sidebar');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('snw-sidebar-overlay');
        if (!btn || !sidebar) return;

        btn.onclick = (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            document.body.classList.toggle('snw-sidebar-open');
        };
        if (overlay) {
            overlay.onclick = () => document.body.classList.remove('snw-sidebar-open');
        }
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') document.body.classList.remove('snw-sidebar-open');
        });
    }

    function bindLang() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-lang');
        if (!btn) return;
        btn.onclick = () => {
            SNW.ToastUtil.info('다국어는 Stage 7에서 지원 예정');
        };
    }

    function bindConnection() {
        const dot = document.getElementById('conn-status');
        if (!dot) return;
        SNW.Store.watch('connection', (conn) => {
            dot.className = 'snw-status-dot snw-status-dot--' + conn;
        });
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 부트
    // =========================================================
    window.SNW_BOOT = async function() {
        if (window.SNW_BOOT_CALLED) return;
        window.SNW_BOOT_CALLED = true;

        try {
            bindSidebar();
            bindLang();
            bindConnection();

            // Now Playing Bar 초기화
            if (SNW.NowPlaying) SNW.NowPlaying.init();

            // Store ↔ WS
            SNW.WsManager.get('state');
            SNW.StoreBridge.start();

            // Router
            SNW.Router.init();

            SNW.LogUtil.info('App bootstrap complete');
        } catch (e) {
            SNW.LogUtil.error('bootstrap failed', e);
        }
    };

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => window.SNW_BOOT());
    } else {
        window.SNW_BOOT();
    }
})(window.SNW);
```

:root {
    --snw-bg: #0f1419;
    --snw-bg-2: #0a0f14;

    --snw-surface-2: #232b36;
    --snw-surface-3: #2f3844;
