3. 프론트엔드 — Simulation (바람 스튜디오)

3.1 135.util.preview.js 확장 — Feelings (5축)

```javascript
// 기존 toFeelings에 freshness 추가

toFeelings(intensity, variability, gustFreq) {
    const clamp = v => Math.max(0, Math.min(5, Math.round(v)));
    return {
        coolness:   clamp(intensity / 20),
        smoothness: clamp(5 - variability / 20),
        dynamism:   clamp((variability + gustFreq) / 40),
        calmness:   clamp(5 - (intensity + gustFreq) / 40),
        freshness:  clamp((intensity + gustFreq) / 40),
    };
},
```

3.2 140.ui.simulation.js — 바람 스튜디오 모달 ★

```javascript
// =========================================================
// 140.ui.simulation.js — Simulation 모달 (5축 레이더 + A/B)
// =========================================================
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
SNW.UI = SNW.UI || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    /**
     * Simulation 모달
     * @param {Object} config {
     *   mode: 'single' | 'ab',
     *   a: { presetCode, styleCode, override },  // 필수
     *   b: { presetCode, styleCode, override },  // mode='ab'일 때
     *   onApply: (chosenConfig) => void,         // "적용" 버튼
     * }
     */
    SNW.UI.simulationModal = async function(config) {
        await SNW.Preview.init();

        // =========================================================
        // A 파형 생성
        // =========================================================
        const waveA = await SNW.Preview.generateStep({
            type: 'PRESET',
            presetCode: config.a.presetCode,
            styleCode:  config.a.styleCode,
            override:   config.a.override || { enabled: false },
        });

        const feelingsA = SNW.Preview.toFeelings(
            waveA.intensity, waveA.variability, waveA.gustFreq);

        // =========================================================
        // B 파형 생성 (A/B 모드)
        // =========================================================
        let waveB = null, feelingsB = null;
        if (config.mode === 'ab' && config.b) {
            waveB = await SNW.Preview.generateStep({
                type: 'PRESET',
                presetCode: config.b.presetCode,
                styleCode:  config.b.styleCode,
                override:   config.b.override || { enabled: false },
            });
            feelingsB = SNW.Preview.toFeelings(
                waveB.intensity, waveB.variability, waveB.gustFreq);
        }

        // =========================================================
        // DOM
        // =========================================================
        const content = document.createElement('div');
        content.className = 'snw-sim';
        content.innerHTML = `
            <div class="snw-sim__tabs">
                <button type="button" class="snw-tab-btn ${config.mode !== 'ab' ? 'is-active' : ''}" data-mode="single">🎬 단일 미리보기</button>
                <button type="button" class="snw-tab-btn ${config.mode === 'ab' ? 'is-active' : ''}" data-mode="ab">⚖ A/B 비교</button>
            </div>

            <div class="snw-sim__wave-section" data-slot="wave-a">
                <div class="snw-sim__wave-header">
                    <span class="snw-sim__badge">${config.mode === 'ab' ? 'A' : '설정'}</span>
                    <span>${config.a.presetCode} · ${config.a.styleCode}</span>
                </div>
                <canvas class="snw-sim__canvas" data-slot="canvas-a" width="600" height="180"></canvas>
                <div class="snw-sim__meta" data-slot="meta-a"></div>
            </div>

            <div class="snw-sim__wave-section" data-slot="wave-b" ${config.mode !== 'ab' ? 'style="display:none"' : ''}>
                <div class="snw-sim__wave-header">
                    <span class="snw-sim__badge snw-sim__badge--b">B</span>
                    <span>${config.b ? config.b.presetCode + ' · ' + config.b.styleCode : '-'}</span>
                </div>
                <canvas class="snw-sim__canvas" data-slot="canvas-b" width="600" height="180"></canvas>
                <div class="snw-sim__meta" data-slot="meta-b"></div>
            </div>

            <div class="snw-sim__feelings">
                <h3>감성 피드백</h3>
                <div class="snw-sim__radar-wrap">
                    <canvas class="snw-sim__radar" data-slot="radar" width="320" height="320"></canvas>
                </div>
                <div class="snw-sim__feelings-legend" data-slot="legend"></div>
            </div>

            <div class="snw-sim__actions">
                <button type="button" class="snw-btn" data-action="close">닫기</button>
                ${config.mode === 'ab'
                    ? `<button type="button" class="snw-btn" data-action="apply-b">B로 적용</button>
                       <button type="button" class="snw-btn snw-btn-primary" data-action="apply-a">A로 적용</button>`
                    : `<button type="button" class="snw-btn snw-btn-primary" data-action="apply-a">✓ 이 설정 적용</button>`}
            </div>
        `;

        // =========================================================
        // 파형 애니메이션
        // =========================================================
        const canvasA = content.querySelector('[data-slot="canvas-a"]');
        const canvasB = content.querySelector('[data-slot="canvas-b"]');
        const radar   = content.querySelector('[data-slot="radar"]');

        animateWave(canvasA, waveA.points, '#4a9eff');
        if (waveB) animateWave(canvasB, waveB.points, '#f0883e');

        // =========================================================
        // 메타 표시
        // =========================================================
        renderMeta(content.querySelector('[data-slot="meta-a"]'), waveA);
        if (waveB) renderMeta(content.querySelector('[data-slot="meta-b"]'), waveB);

        // =========================================================
        // 5축 레이더
        // =========================================================
        drawRadar(radar, feelingsA, feelingsB);

        // 범례
        const legend = content.querySelector('[data-slot="legend"]');
        legend.innerHTML = `
            <div class="snw-sim__legend-item">
                <span class="snw-sim__legend-dot" style="background:#4a9eff"></span>
                ${config.mode === 'ab' ? 'A' : '현재'}
            </div>
            ${config.mode === 'ab' ? `
                <div class="snw-sim__legend-item">
                    <span class="snw-sim__legend-dot" style="background:#f0883e"></span>
                    B
                </div>
            ` : ''}
        `;

        // =========================================================
        // 액션
        // =========================================================
        let resolved = false;
        let promiseResolve;

        const promise = new Promise(res => { promiseResolve = res; });

        const close = (chosen) => {
            if (resolved) return;
            resolved = true;
            modalRef.close();
            promiseResolve(chosen);
        };

        const modalRef = SNW.UI.modal(
            '🎬 바람 스튜디오', content,
            null,   // OK 자체 처리
            null,
            { hideOk: true, hideCancel: true, width: '600px' }
        );

        content.querySelector('[data-action="close"]').onclick = () => close(null);
        content.querySelector('[data-action="apply-a"]').onclick = () => close(config.a);
        if (config.mode === 'ab') {
            content.querySelector('[data-action="apply-b"]').onclick = () => close(config.b);
        }

        // 탭 전환 (A/B)
        content.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach(btn => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                const mode = btn.getAttribute('data-mode');
                content.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach(b =>
                    b.classList.toggle('is-active', b === btn));
                const wb = content.querySelector('[data-slot="wave-b"]');
                if (wb) wb.style.display = (mode === 'ab') ? 'block' : 'none';

                // 레이더 재렌더
                drawRadar(radar,
                    feelingsA,
                    (mode === 'ab') ? feelingsB : null);
            };
        });

        return promise;
    };

    // =========================================================
    // 내부: 파형 애니메이션
    // =========================================================
    function animateWave(canvas, points, color) {
        if (!canvas || !points || points.length === 0) return;

        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        const w = canvas.width;
        const h = canvas.height;

        let minV = Infinity, maxV = -Infinity;
        points.forEach(p => {
            if (p.wind < minV) minV = p.wind;
            if (p.wind > maxV) maxV = p.wind;
        });
        if (maxV <= minV) maxV = minV + 1;

        let drawn = 0;
        const step = 3;

        const drawNext = () => {
            if (drawn >= points.length || !canvas.isConnected) return;

            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

            // 그리드
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(139,148,158,0.1)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 1;
            for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
                const y = (h / 4) * i;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(0, y);
                ctx.lineTo(w, y);
                ctx.stroke();
            }

            // 파형
            ctx.strokeStyle = color;
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
            ctx.beginPath();
            for (let i = 0; i <= drawn; i++) {
                const p = points[i];
                const x = (i / (points.length - 1)) * w;
                const y = h - ((p.wind - minV) / (maxV - minV)) * (h - 20) - 10;
                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
                else         ctx.lineTo(x, y);
            }
            ctx.stroke();

            // 돌풍 마커
            for (let i = 0; i <= drawn; i++) {
                if (!points[i].gust) continue;
                const x = (i / (points.length - 1)) * w;
                const y = h - ((points[i].wind - minV) / (maxV - minV)) * (h - 20) - 10;
                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(240,136,62,0.9)';
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(x, y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fill();
            }

            drawn += step;
            setTimeout(drawNext, 20);
        };
        drawNext();
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 내부: 메타 표시
    // =========================================================
    function renderMeta(el, wave) {
        if (!el || !wave) return;
        el.innerHTML = `
            <div>평균 <b>${wave.avg.toFixed(1)} m/s</b></div>
            <div>강도 <b>${Math.round(wave.intensity)}%</b></div>
            <div>변동성 <b>${Math.round(wave.variability)}%</b></div>
            <div>돌풍 <b>${Math.round(wave.gustFreq)}%</b></div>
        `;
    }

    // =========================================================
    // 내부: 5축 레이더
    // =========================================================
    function drawRadar(canvas, fA, fB) {
        if (!canvas || !fA) return;

        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        const w = canvas.width;
        const h = canvas.height;
        const cx = w / 2;
        const cy = h / 2;
        const maxR = Math.min(w, h) / 2 - 40;

        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

        const axes = [
            { key: 'coolness',   label: '시원함',   angle: -Math.PI / 2 },
            { key: 'freshness',  label: '청량감',   angle: -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 / 5 },
            { key: 'dynamism',   label: '역동성',   angle: -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 4 / 5 },
            { key: 'smoothness', label: '부드러움', angle: -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 6 / 5 },
            { key: 'calmness',   label: '차분함',   angle: -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 8 / 5 },
        ];

        // 그리드 (5단계 원)
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(139,148,158,0.15)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        for (let ring = 1; ring <= 5; ring++) {
            const r = (ring / 5) * maxR;
            ctx.beginPath();
            axes.forEach((axis, i) => {
                const x = cx + Math.cos(axis.angle) * r;
                const y = cy + Math.sin(axis.angle) * r;
                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
                else         ctx.lineTo(x, y);
            });
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.stroke();
        }

        // 축
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(139,148,158,0.3)';
        axes.forEach(axis => {
            const x = cx + Math.cos(axis.angle) * maxR;
            const y = cy + Math.sin(axis.angle) * maxR;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
            ctx.lineTo(x, y);
            ctx.stroke();
        });

        // 라벨
        ctx.fillStyle = '#8b949e';
        ctx.font = '11px sans-serif';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        axes.forEach(axis => {
            const x = cx + Math.cos(axis.angle) * (maxR + 20);
            const y = cy + Math.sin(axis.angle) * (maxR + 20);
            ctx.fillText(axis.label, x, y);
        });

        // A 폴리곤
        drawPolygon(ctx, cx, cy, maxR, axes, fA, 'rgba(74,158,255,0.35)', 'rgba(74,158,255,1)');

        // B 폴리곤
        if (fB) {
            drawPolygon(ctx, cx, cy, maxR, axes, fB, 'rgba(240,136,62,0.25)', 'rgba(240,136,62,1)');
        }

        // 중심점
        ctx.fillStyle = '#4a9eff';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
    }

    function drawPolygon(ctx, cx, cy, maxR, axes, feelings, fillColor, strokeColor) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        axes.forEach((axis, i) => {
            const val = feelings[axis.key] || 0;
            const r = (val / 5) * maxR;
            const x = cx + Math.cos(axis.angle) * r;
            const y = cy + Math.sin(axis.angle) * r;
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
            else         ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        });
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fillStyle = fillColor;
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = strokeColor;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.stroke();
    }
})(window.SNW);
```

4. 프론트엔드 — Chart 회고

4.1 260.page.monitor.js 재작성 — 회고 탭

```javascript
// =========================================================
// 260.page.monitor.js — Monitor (실시간 + 회고 탭)
// =========================================================
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
SNW.Page = SNW.Page || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    SNW.Page.Monitor = class Monitor {
        constructor(container) {
            this.el = container;
            this._active = true;
            this._currentTab = 'live';
            this._unsubs = [];
            this._liveEq = null;
            this._retroRange = '24h';
        }

        async mount() {
            this.el.innerHTML = '';
            this._render();
            this._bindStore();
            await this._loadLiveData();

            // 라이브 이퀄라이저 초기화
            const liveEqSlot = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="live-eq"]');
            if (liveEqSlot) {
                this._liveEq = SNW.UI.equalizer(liveEqSlot, { bars: 40, fps: 30, maxWind: 11 });
            }

            // 탭 초기화
            this._switchTab(this._currentTab);
        }

        unmount() {
            this._active = false;
            this._unsubs.forEach(fn => { try { fn(); } catch {} });
            this._unsubs = [];
            if (this._liveEq) this._liveEq.stop();
        }

        _render() {
            this.el.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-monitor">
                    <div class="snw-tabs">
                        <button type="button" class="snw-tab-btn is-active" data-tab="live">📊 실시간</button>
                        <button type="button" class="snw-tab-btn" data-tab="retro">🎬 회고</button>
                    </div>
                    <div data-tab-content="live"></div>
                    <div data-tab-content="retro" style="display:none;"></div>
                </div>`;

            this.el.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach(btn => {
                btn.onclick = () => this._switchTab(btn.getAttribute('data-tab'));
            });
        }

        _switchTab(tab) {
            this._currentTab = tab;
            this.el.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach(b =>
                b.classList.toggle('is-active', b.getAttribute('data-tab') === tab));
            this.el.querySelectorAll('[data-tab-content]').forEach(c => {
                c.style.display = (c.getAttribute('data-tab-content') === tab) ? 'block' : 'none';
            });

            if (tab === 'live')  this._renderLive();
            if (tab === 'retro') this._renderRetro();
        }

        // =========================================================
        // 라이브 탭
        // =========================================================
        _renderLive() {
            const slot = this.el.querySelector('[data-tab-content="live"]');
            slot.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-card">
                    <h2>📊 실시간 풍속</h2>
                    <div data-slot="live-eq" class="snw-monitor__eq" style="height:100px;"></div>
                    <div class="snw-monitor__live-info" data-slot="live-info"></div>
                </div>
                <div class="snw-card">
                    <h2>🌡️ 센서</h2>
                    <div class="snw-monitor__sensors" data-slot="live-sensors"></div>
                </div>`;

            // 이퀄라이저 재초기화
            const eqSlot = slot.querySelector('[data-slot="live-eq"]');
            this._liveEq = SNW.UI.equalizer(eqSlot, { bars: 40, fps: 30, maxWind: 11 });
            this._liveEq.start();
            this._onLiveUpdate();
        }

        _onLiveUpdate() {
            const ctl = SNW.Store.get().control;
            const sim = SNW.Store.get().sim;
            const session = SNW.Store.get().session;

            if (this._liveEq && sim) {
                this._liveEq.push(sim.windSpeed || 0);
            }

            const infoEl = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="live-info"]');
            if (infoEl && session && session.type !== 'NONE') {
                let title = '';
                if (session.type === 'PLAYLIST') title = session.profileName;
                else if (session.type === 'PRESET') title = `${session.presetCode} · ${session.styleCode}`;
                else title = `일반 선풍기 ${Math.round(session.percent || 0)}%`;

                infoEl.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="snw-monitor__live-row">
                        <span>${session.trigger === 'MANUAL' ? '✋' : '⏰'} ${SNW.DomUtil.escape(title)}</span>
                    </div>
                    ${session.type === 'PLAYLIST' ? `
                        <div class="snw-monitor__live-row">
                            <span>Step ${session.currentStep}/${session.totalSteps}</span>
                            <span>${this._fmtTime(session.stepRemainSec || 0)}</span>
                        </div>` : ''}
                `;
            } else if (infoEl) {
                infoEl.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">세션 없음</div>';
            }

            const sensorsEl = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="live-sensors"]');
            if (sensorsEl && ctl && ctl.sensor) {
                const s = ctl.sensor;
                sensorsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="snw-sensor-row">
                        <span>🌡️ 온도</span><b>${(s.tempC || 0).toFixed(1)}°C</b>
                    </div>
                    <div class="snw-sensor-row">
                        <span>💧 습도</span><b>${Math.round(s.humidity || 0)}%</b>
                    </div>
                    <div class="snw-sensor-row">
                        <span>👤 재실</span><b>${s.motionActive ? '감지' : '부재'}</b>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }

        // =========================================================
        // 회고 탭
        // =========================================================
        async _renderRetro() {
            const slot = this.el.querySelector('[data-tab-content="retro"]');
            slot.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-card">
                    <h2>🎬 세션 회고</h2>
                    <div class="snw-toolbar" style="margin-bottom:12px;">
                        <select data-slot="retro-range">
                            <option value="1h">최근 1시간</option>
                            <option value="24h" selected>최근 24시간</option>
                            <option value="7d">최근 7일</option>
                        </select>
                        <button type="button" class="snw-btn" data-action="refresh">🔄</button>
                    </div>
                    <div data-slot="retro-content" class="snw-loading">로딩 중...</div>
                </div>`;

            slot.querySelector('[data-action="refresh"]').onclick = () => this._loadRetro();
            slot.querySelector('[data-slot="retro-range"]').onchange = () => this._loadRetro();

            await this._loadRetro();
        }

        async _loadRetro() {
            const slot = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="retro-content"]');
            if (!slot) return;

            const rangeEl = this.el.querySelector('[data-slot="retro-range"]');
            const range = rangeEl ? rangeEl.value : '24h';

            const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
            const durMap = { '1h': 3600, '24h': 86400, '7d': 604800 };
            const since = now - (durMap[range] || 86400);

            try {
                const [timeline, summary] = await Promise.all([
                    SNW.Api.getTimeline(since),
                    SNW.Api.getSessionSummary(since, now).catch(() => null),
                ]);

                slot.innerHTML = '';
                slot.appendChild(this._renderSummary(summary));
                slot.appendChild(this._renderTimeline(timeline));
                slot.appendChild(this._renderEventList(timeline));
            } catch (e) {
                slot.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-error">회고 데이터 로드 실패</div>';
            }
        }

        _renderSummary(summary) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'snw-retro-summary';
            if (!summary || !summary.sessions) {
                div.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">세션 데이터 없음</div>';
                return div;
            }

            const stats = summary.stats || {};
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-retro-summary__title">세션 요약</div>
                <div class="snw-retro-summary__grid">
                    <div><span>총 세션</span><b>${stats.totalSessions || 0}회</b></div>
                    <div><span>총 가동</span><b>${this._fmtDuration(stats.totalActiveSec || 0)}</b></div>
                    <div><span>평균 풍속</span><b>${(stats.avgWindSpeed || 0).toFixed(1)} m/s</b></div>
                    <div><span>최고 풍속</span><b>${(stats.maxWindSpeed || 0).toFixed(1)} m/s</b></div>
                    <div><span>평균 온도</span><b>${(stats.avgTempC || 0).toFixed(1)}°C</b></div>
                    <div><span>재실 비율</span><b>${Math.round((stats.presenceRatio || 0) * 100)}%</b></div>
                </div>
            `;
            return div;
        }

        _renderTimeline(timeline) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'snw-retro-timeline';

            if (!timeline || !timeline.events || timeline.events.length === 0) {
                div.innerHTML = '<div class="snw-empty">이벤트 없음</div>';
                return div;
            }

            // 이벤트 마커
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-retro-timeline__title">이벤트 타임라인</div>
                <div class="snw-retro-timeline__bar">
                    ${timeline.events.map(e => this._eventMarker(e, timeline)).join('')}
                </div>
            `;
            return div;
        }

        _eventMarker(e, timeline) {
            const start = timeline.from || 0;
            const end   = timeline.to   || Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
            const span  = Math.max(1, end - start);
            const pos   = ((e.t - start) / span) * 100;

            const icon = {
                0: '⚪',   // STATE_CHANGE
                1: '➡️',   // STEP_CHANGE
                2: '▶',    // SESSION_START
                3: '⏹',    // SESSION_END
                4: '✋',   // MANUAL_OVERRIDE
                5: '🌙',   // AUTOOFF
                6: '⏸',    // PAUSE
                7: '▶',    // RESUME
            }[e.eventType] || '•';

            const color = {
                0: '#59636e',
                1: '#4a9eff',
                2: '#3fb950',
                3: '#8b949e',
                4: '#d29922',
                5: '#f0883e',
                6: '#d29922',
                7: '#3fb950',
            }[e.eventType] || '#59636e';

            return `
                <div class="snw-retro-marker" style="left:${pos}%;background:${color};" title="${this._fmtTimeOfDay(e.t)} · ${SNW.DomUtil.escape(e.label || '')}">
                    <span>${icon}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        _renderEventList(timeline) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'snw-retro-events';

            if (!timeline || !timeline.events || timeline.events.length === 0) {
                return div;
            }

            const events = timeline.events.slice(-30).reverse();
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-retro-events__title">이벤트 목록 (최근 30개)</div>
                ${events.map(e => `
                    <div class="snw-retro-event-row">
                        <span class="snw-retro-event-row__time">${this._fmtTimeOfDay(e.t)}</span>
                        <span class="snw-retro-event-row__icon">${
                            2 === e.eventType ? '▶' :
                            3 === e.eventType ? '⏹' :
                            1 === e.eventType ? '➡️' :
                            4 === e.eventType ? '✋' :
                            5 === e.eventType ? '🌙' :
                            6 === e.eventType ? '⏸' :
                            7 === e.eventType ? '▶' : '•'
                        }</span>
                        <span class="snw-retro-event-row__label">${SNW.DomUtil.escape(e.label || '')}</span>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
            `;
            return div;
        }

        // =========================================================
        // Store
        // =========================================================
        _bindStore() {
            this._unsubs.push(
                SNW.Store.subscribe(() => {
                    if (this._active && this._currentTab === 'live') {
                        this._onLiveUpdate();
                    }
                })
            );
        }

        _fmtTime(sec) {
            if (!sec || sec < 0) return '0:00';
            const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
            const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
            return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        }

        _fmtDuration(sec) {
            if (!sec) return '0분';
            const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
            const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
            return h > 0 ? `${h}시간 ${m}분` : `${m}분`;
        }

        _fmtTimeOfDay(epoch) {
            const d = new Date(epoch * 1000);
            return `${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        }

        async _loadLiveData() {
            try {
                const st = await SNW.Api.getState();
                SNW.Store.set({
                    control: st.control,
                    sim: st.sim,
                    session: st.control?.session,
                });
            } catch (e) {
                SNW.LogUtil.warn('monitor load failed', e);
            }
        }
    };
})(window.SNW);
```

5. 온보딩

5.1 998.onboarding.js

```javascript
// =========================================================
// 998.onboarding.js — 첫 방문 온보딩 (4단계)
// =========================================================
window.SNW = window.SNW || {};
(function(SNW) {
    'use strict';

    const LS_KEY = 'snw.onboarded';

    SNW.Onboarding = {
        async start() {
            // 이미 완료
            if (localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY) === '1') return;

            // =========================================================
            // 1) 언어
            // =========================================================
            const lang = await this._stepLang();
            if (lang) SNW.I18n.setLang(lang);

            // =========================================================
            // 2) WiFi 안내 (이미 연결됨 가정)
            // =========================================================
            await this._stepWifi();
            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));

            // =========================================================
            // 3) 첫 바람 선택 (큐레이션 3개)
            // =========================================================
            const choice = await this._stepFirstBreeze();

            // =========================================================
            // 4) 지금 시작?
            // =========================================================
            if (choice) {
                const startNow = await this._stepStartNow(choice);
                if (startNow) {
                    try {
                        await SNW.Api.startPreset(choice.presetCode, choice.styleCode);
                    } catch (e) {
                        SNW.LogUtil.warn('onboarding start failed', e);
                    }
                }
            }

            // 완료
            localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, '1');
        },

        // =========================================================
        // 1) 언어 선택
        // =========================================================
        _stepLang() {
            const content = document.createElement('div');
            content.className = 'snw-onboarding-step';
            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-onboarding-hero">🌿</div>
                <h2>Smart Nature Wind</h2>
                <p class="snw-onboarding-desc">
                    자연의 바람을 그대로 담은 스마트 선풍기<br>
                    A smart fan that recreates nature's breeze
                </p>
                <div class="snw-onboarding-choices">
                    <button type="button" class="snw-onboarding-choice" data-lang="ko">
                        <span class="snw-onboarding-choice__icon">🇰🇷</span>
                        <span>한국어</span>
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" class="snw-onboarding-choice" data-lang="en">
                        <span class="snw-onboarding-choice__icon">🇬🇧</span>
                        <span>English</span>
                    </button>
                </div>
            `;

            return new Promise(resolve => {
                content.querySelectorAll('[data-lang]').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.onclick = () => {
                        resolve(btn.getAttribute('data-lang'));
                        modalRef.close();
                    };
                });
                const modalRef = SNW.UI.modal('환영합니다 / Welcome', content, null, null,
                    { hideOk: true, hideCancel: true, width: '440px' });
            });
        },

        // =========================================================
        // 2) WiFi 안내
        // =========================================================
        _stepWifi() {
            const content = document.createElement('div');
            content.className = 'snw-onboarding-step';
            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-onboarding-hero">📶</div>
                <h2>${SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.wifiTitle')}</h2>
                <p class="snw-onboarding-desc">
                    ${SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.wifiDesc')}
                </p>
                <div class="snw-onboarding-info">
                    <div><b>SSID:</b> NatureWind</div>
                    <div><b>Password:</b> 2540</div>
                    <div><b>URL:</b> http://192.168.4.1</div>
                </div>
            `;

            return new Promise(resolve => {
                const modalRef = SNW.UI.modal(
                    SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.step2'),
                    content,
                    () => { resolve(); return true; },
                    null,
                    { okLabel: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.connected'), hideCancel: true, width: '440px' }
                );
            });
        },

        // =========================================================
        // 3) 첫 바람 선택
        // =========================================================
        _stepFirstBreeze() {
            const choices = [
                {
                    presetCode: 'OCEAN', styleCode: 'BALANCE',
                    icon: '🌊',
                    title: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.breeze.ocean'),
                    desc: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.breeze.oceanDesc'),
                },
                {
                    presetCode: 'FOREST_CANOPY', styleCode: 'RELAX',
                    icon: '🌲',
                    title: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.breeze.forest'),
                    desc: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.breeze.forestDesc'),
                },
                {
                    presetCode: 'MOUNTAIN', styleCode: 'ACTIVE',
                    icon: '🏔️',
                    title: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.breeze.mountain'),
                    desc: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.breeze.mountainDesc'),
                },
            ];

            const content = document.createElement('div');
            content.className = 'snw-onboarding-step';
            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-onboarding-hero">🍃</div>
                <h2>${SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.firstBreezeTitle')}</h2>
                <p class="snw-onboarding-desc">${SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.firstBreezeDesc')}</p>
                <div class="snw-onboarding-breeze-choices">
                    ${choices.map((c, i) => `
                        <button type="button" class="snw-breeze-choice" data-idx="${i}">
                            <div class="snw-breeze-choice__icon">${c.icon}</div>
                            <div class="snw-breeze-choice__title">${c.title}</div>
                            <div class="snw-breeze-choice__desc">${c.desc}</div>
                        </button>
                    `).join('')}
                </div>
            `;

            return new Promise(resolve => {
                content.querySelectorAll('[data-idx]').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.onclick = () => {
                        const idx = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-idx'));
                        resolve(choices[idx]);
                        modalRef.close();
                    };
                });
                const modalRef = SNW.UI.modal(
                    SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.step3'),
                    content, null, null,
                    { hideOk: true, hideCancel: true, width: '480px' }
                );
            });
        },

        // =========================================================
        // 4) 지금 시작?
        // =========================================================
        _stepStartNow(choice) {
            const content = document.createElement('div');
            content.className = 'snw-onboarding-step';
            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="snw-onboarding-hero">${choice.icon || '🍃'}</div>
                <h2>${SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.startNowTitle')}</h2>
                <p class="snw-onboarding-desc">
                    ${SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.startNowDesc', { name: choice.title })}
                </p>
            `;

            return new Promise(resolve => {
                const modalRef = SNW.UI.modal(
                    SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.step4'),
                    content,
                    () => { resolve(true); return true; },
                    () => { resolve(false); },
                    {
                        okLabel: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.startNow'),
                        cancelLabel: SNW.I18n.t('onboarding.later'),
                        width: '400px',
                    }
                );
            });
        },
    };
})(window.SNW);
```

5.2 999.app.js — 온보딩 통합

```javascript
// SNW_BOOT 끝에 추가
setTimeout(() => {
    SNW.Onboarding?.start();
}, 500);
```

```javascript
            // =====================================================

            'monitor.title':        'Monitor',
            'monitor.liveTab':      '📊 Live',
            'monitor.retroTab':     '🎬 Retro',
            'monitor.liveWind':     '📊 Live Wind',
            'monitor.sensors':      '🌡️ Sensors',
            'monitor.retroTitle':   '🎬 Session Retro',
            'monitor.range1h':      'Last 1h',
            'monitor.range24h':     'Last 24h',
            'monitor.range7d':      'Last 7d',
            'monitor.refresh':      '🔄',
            'monitor.sessionSummary': 'Session Summary',
            'monitor.totalSessions': 'Sessions',
            'monitor.totalActive':  'Active Time',
            'monitor.avgWind':      'Avg Wind',
            'monitor.maxWind':      'Max Wind',
            'monitor.avgTemp':      'Avg Temp',
            'monitor.presenceRatio': 'Presence',
            'monitor.timelineTitle': 'Event Timeline',
            'monitor.eventList':    'Events (Latest 30)',
            'monitor.temp':         'Temp',
            'monitor.humidity':     'Humidity',
            'monitor.motion':       'Motion',
            'monitor.detected':     'Detected',
            'monitor.absent':       'Absent',

            // =====================================================
            // Context
            // =====================================================
            'context.comfort':      'Comfortable',
            'context.slight':       'A bit warm',
            'context.uncomfort':    'Sticky weather',
            'context.severe':       'Suffocating',

            // =====================================================
            // Settings
            // =====================================================
            'settings.title':       '⚙️ Settings',
            'settings.tabGeneral':  'General',
            'settings.tabNetwork':  'Network',
            'settings.tabHardware': 'Hardware',
            'settings.tabAdvanced': 'Advanced',
            'settings.lang':        'Language',
            'settings.version':     'Version',
            'settings.manual':      'User Manual',
            'settings.logs':        'System Logs',
            'settings.diag':        'Diagnostics',
            'settings.firmware':    'Firmware Update',
            'settings.factoryReset':'Factory Reset',
            'settings.advanced':    '⚠️ Advanced',

            // =====================================================
            // Header / Nav
            // =====================================================
            'nav.home':         'Home',
            'nav.wind':         'Wind',
            'nav.automation':   'Auto',
            'nav.monitor':      'Monitor',
            'nav.settings':     'Settings',
            'header.menu':      'Menu',
            'header.language':  'Language',
            'header.online':    'Online',
            'header.offline':   'Offline',
            'header.connecting':'Connecting',

            // =====================================================
            // Messages
            // =====================================================
            'msg.saveSuccess':      'Saved',
            'msg.deleteSuccess':    'Deleted',
            'msg.updateSuccess':    'Updated',
            'msg.playSuccess':      'Playing',
            'msg.loadFailed':       'Load failed',
            'msg.saveFailed':       'Save failed',
            'msg.networkError':     'Network error',
            'msg.stepMin':          'At least 1 step required',
            'msg.stepMax':          'Maximum 8 steps allowed',
            'msg.nameRequired':     'Please enter a name',
            'msg.durationRange':    'Duration must be 1~1440 min',
            'msg.selectProfile':    'Please select a playlist',
            'msg.timeOrder':        'End time must be after start time',
        },
    };

    const SUPPORTED_LANGS = ['ko', 'en'];

    let currentLang = (function() {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(SNW.CFG?.LS_KEY?.LANG || 'snw.lang');
            if (SUPPORTED_LANGS.includes(saved)) return saved;
            // 브라우저 언어 자동 감지
            const nav = navigator.language || 'ko';
            return nav.startsWith('ko') ? 'ko' : 'en';
        } catch { return 'ko'; }
    })();

    SNW.I18n = {
        getLang() { return currentLang; },

        setLang(lang) {
            if (!SUPPORTED_LANGS.includes(lang)) return;
            currentLang = lang;
            try { localStorage.setItem(SNW.CFG?.LS_KEY?.LANG || 'snw.lang', lang); } catch {}
            document.documentElement.lang = lang;
            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('snw:langchange', { detail: { lang } }));
            this.applyTo(document);
        },

        t(key, params) {
            const dict = DICT[currentLang] || DICT.ko;
            let s = dict[key] != null ? dict[key] : (DICT.ko[key] || key);
            if (params) {
                s = s.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, k) => params[k] != null ? String(params[k]) : m);
            }
            return s;
        },

        hasKey(key) {
            return DICT.ko[key] != null || DICT.en[key] != null;
        },

        applyTo(root) {
            (root || document).querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
                const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
                if (key && this.hasKey(key)) el.textContent = this.t(key);
            });
        },
    };

    document.documentElement.lang = currentLang;
})(window.SNW);
```

7. CSS

7.1 _simulation.css

```css
.snw-sim {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--snw-spacing);
    max-height: 80vh;
}

.snw-sim__tabs {
    display: flex;
    gap: 4px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--snw-border);
    padding-bottom: 8px;
}
.snw-sim__tabs .snw-tab-btn {
    padding: 8px 14px;
}

.snw-sim__wave-section {
    background: var(--snw-bg-2);
    border-radius: var(--snw-radius);
    padding: 12px;
}

.snw-sim__wave-header {
    display: flex;
    gap: 8px;
    align-items: center;
    font-size: 13px;
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
    margin-bottom: 8px;
}

.snw-sim__badge {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    width: 22px; height: 22px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: var(--snw-primary);
    color: white;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 700;
}
.snw-sim__badge--b { background: #f0883e; }

.snw-sim__canvas {
    width: 100%;
    height: 180px;
    display: block;
    border-radius: var(--snw-radius-sm);
}

.snw-sim__meta {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: 8px;
    margin-top: 8px;
    font-size: 11px;
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
}
.snw-sim__meta b { color: var(--snw-text); }

.snw-sim__feelings h3 {
    font-size: 13px;
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
    margin-bottom: 8px;
}

.snw-sim__radar-wrap {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
}

.snw-sim__radar {
    max-width: 320px;
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
}

.snw-sim__feelings-legend {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 16px;
    font-size: 12px;
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
    margin-top: 8px;
}

.snw-sim__legend-item {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
}

.snw-sim__legend-dot {
    width: 10px;
    height: 10px;
    border-radius: 50%;
}

.snw-sim__actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: 8px;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    padding-top: 8px;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--snw-border);
    flex-wrap: wrap;
}
```

7.2 _monitor.css

```css
.snw-monitor {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--snw-spacing);
}

.snw-monitor__eq {
    background: var(--snw-bg-2);
    border-radius: var(--snw-radius);
    overflow: hidden;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.snw-monitor__live-info {
    font-size: 13px;
    color: var(--snw-text);
}

.snw-monitor__live-row {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    padding: 4px 0;
}

.snw-monitor__sensors {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 8px;
}

.snw-sensor-row {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    font-size: 13px;
    color: var(--snw-text);
}

/* 회고 */
.snw-retro-summary {
    background: var(--snw-surface-2);
    border-radius: var(--snw-radius);
    padding: 12px;
    margin-bottom: 16px;
}
.snw-retro-summary__title {
    font-size: 14px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--snw-text);
    margin-bottom: 12px;
}
.snw-retro-summary__grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    gap: 8px;
    font-size: 12px;
}
.snw-retro-summary__grid > div {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    padding: 4px 0;
}
.snw-retro-summary__grid span {
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
}

.snw-retro-timeline {
    margin-bottom: 16px;
}
.snw-retro-timeline__title {
    font-size: 13px;
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
    margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.snw-retro-timeline__bar {
    position: relative;
    height: 40px;
    background: var(--snw-bg-2);
    border-radius: var(--snw-radius);
    overflow: hidden;
}
.snw-retro-marker {
    position: absolute;
    top: 4px;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    width: 24px; height: 24px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-size: 11px;
    color: white;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: transform 0.15s;
}
.snw-retro-marker:hover {
    transform: translateX(-50%) scale(1.2);
    z-index: 2;
}

.snw-retro-events__title {
    font-size: 13px;
    color: var(--snw-text-dim);
    margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.snw-retro-event-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    padding: 4px 8px;
    font-size: 12px;
    color: var(--snw-text);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--snw-border);
}
.snw-retro-event-row__time {
    color: var(--snw-text-faint);
    font-family: var(--snw-font-mono);
    min-width: 48px;
}
.snw-retro-event-row__icon {
    min-width: 20px;
    text-align: center;
}
.snw-retro-event-row__label {
    flex: 1;
    color: var(--snw-text);
}
```

8. Stage 7 검증 체크리스트

A. 백엔드

☐ GET /api/v0203/monitor/timeline?since=<epoch> → 200 (events 배열)
☐ GET /api/v0203/monitor/session-summary?from=<>&to=<> → 200
☐ CT10_Monitor::pushSessionEvent 정상 (로그 확인)
☐ 세션 시작/종료/Step 전환/Pause/Resume 이벤트 캡처

B. Simulation 모달

☐ Studio의 [🎬] → 모달 열림
☐ 파형 애니메이션 (A)
☐ A/B 탭 전환 → 두 파형 동시 표시
☐ 5축 레이더 (시원함/청량감/역동성/부드러움/차분함)
☐ A/B 오버레이 레이더 (파랑 vs 주황)
☐ "이 설정 적용" → Session 시작 (또는 Studio에 반영)

C. Chart 회고

☐ Monitor 접속 → 실시간/회고 탭
☐ 회고 탭 → 범위 선택 (1h/24h/7d)
☐ 세션 요약 (총 세션/가동 시간/평균 풍속)
☐ 이벤트 타임라인 (마커 표시)
☐ 이벤트 목록 (시간/아이콘/라벨)
☐ 새로고침 → 데이터 갱신

D. 온보딩

☐ localStorage.snw.onboarded 삭제 → 새로고침
☐ 1단계: 언어 선택 (ko/en)
☐ 2단계: WiFi 안내
☐ 3단계: 첫 바람 (바다/숲/산)
☐ 4단계: 지금 시작? → Session 시작
☐ 완료 → localStorage.onboarded = 1
☐ 재방문 시 온보딩 스킵

E. 다국어

☐ ko → en 전환 → 모든 페이지 즉시 반영
☐ en → ko 복귀 → 유지
☐ 모든 라벨/메시지 번역 완료
☐ snw:langchange 이벤트 정상

F. 전체 E2E 시나리오

시나리오: 첫 방문 → 종합 사용

1. 첫 방문: http://[IP]/ 접속
2. 온보딩: 언어 (ko) → WiFi 안내 → "바다" 선택 → 지금 시작
3. 첫 감동: Now Playing Bar에 "OCEAN · BALANCE", 이퀄라이저 애니메이션
4. 홈 탐색: 이퀄라이저, 즐겨찾기, Context 추천("강한 계곡풍"), 일반 선풍기
5. Studio: 새 프로필 "저녁 시원함" → Step 3개 (숲속 20분 + 휴지 5분 + 바다 30분) → [🎬] 미리보기 → 저장
6. Playlist: "저녁 시원함" 카드 확인 → [▶ 재생]
7. 회고: Monitor → 회고 탭 → 세션 요약 + 이벤트 마커
8. Schedule: "저녁 시원함"을 스케줄에 등록
9. Manual 개입: 수동 3단 → 배지 ⏰ 08:00 strike → 모달 → 수동 정지
10. 다국어: en 전환 → 모든 UI 영어

G. 회귀 (Stage 1~6)

☐ Studio/Playlist 정상
☐ Schedule 정상
☐ Session 일시정지/재개 정상
☐ 이퀄라이저 애니메이션 정상
☐ Snap 슬라이더 정상
☐ Context 추천 정상
☐ Manual override 정상

9. Stage 7 완료 후 상태

이 시점에 가능한 것 (전체 기능)

· 온보딩: 4단계 (30초 이내 첫 바람)
· Studio: Step 작곡 + Simulation 미리보기
· Playlist: 프로필 관리 + 원터치 재생
· Normal Fan: Snap + Haptic
· Schedule: 자동 실행 + 참조 무결성
· Manual 우선: 스케줄 충돌 인지
· Visualizer: 이퀄라이저 + Sparkline
· Context: DI 기반 추천
· Chart: 회고 (의도 vs 실제 + 이벤트 마커)
· Now Playing: 전역 미니 플레이어
· 다국어: ko/en 완전판

v0203 최종 완성

7 Stage 로드맵 완료 — 8주 (Stage 1주 + 1.5주 + 1주 + 1.5주 + 1주 + 1.5주 + 1.5주)

10. 최종 통합 검증 (릴리스 체크리스트)

KPI 검증 (실사용 5명)

지표 목표 측정 방법
첫 바람까지 시간 < 30초 온보딩 로그 (시작 → Session 시작)
온보딩 완료율 80% localStorage.onboarded=1 비율
일일 탭 수 < 5회 이벤트 로깅
홈 체류 비중 60% GA4 (또는 로컬 로깅)
Pause 사용률 20% (주 1회+) 이벤트 로깅
Snap 오터치율 < 5% Snap 이외 값 진입 빈도
Context 추천 수락률 25% 추천 카드 → 재생
NPS 50 설문
이퀄라이저 60fps 5초 이상 유지 성능 측정

릴리스 체크리스트

빌드:

☐ pio run --environment esp32-s3-n16r8 SUCCESS
☐ pio run --target buildfs SUCCESS (Gzip 생성)
☐ 링크 무결성 (undefined/multiple 0건)
☐ Flash/RAM 사용량 < 50%

기능:

☐ Stage 1~7 체크리스트 100% 통과
☐ 회귀 0건
☐ 크리티컬 버그 0건

문서:

☐ README_0203.md (Stage별 요약)
☐ UserManual_0203.md (v0202 갱신)
☐ API 문서 (자동 생성)

사용자 매뉴얼:

☐ 온보딩 스크린샷
☐ Studio 작곡 가이드
☐ Schedule 설정 가이드
☐ 문제 해결 FAQ

릴리스 태그:

```bash
git tag -a v0203-release-$(date +%Y-%m-%d) -m "v0203 Release (Stage 7 완료)"
git push origin v0203-release-$(date +%Y-%m-%d)
```

11. v0203 최종 요약 (전체 8주)

기능 완성도

```
🎨 Wind Design Studio     ✅ (Stage 4)
🎵 Wind Playlist          ✅ (Stage 4)
🌀 Normal Fan             ✅ (Stage 4 + Stage 6 Snap)
⏰ Schedule 자동화        ✅ (Stage 5)
▶ Playback Session        ✅ (Stage 2)
✨ 이퀄라이저              ✅ (Stage 6)
📳 Snap + Haptic          ✅ (Stage 6)
💡 Context 추천           ✅ (Stage 6)
🎬 Simulation             ✅ (Stage 7)
📊 Chart 회고             ✅ (Stage 7)
🌐 다국어 (ko/en)          ✅ (Stage 7)
🎁 온보딩                 ✅ (Stage 7)
```

Stage별 소요 주차

Stage 기간 누적
1 1주 1주
2 1.5주 2.5주
3 1주 3.5주
4 1.5주 5주
5 1주 6주
6 1.5주 7.5주
7 1.5주 8주

최종 사용자 여정 (30초 감동)

```
박스 개봉 → 전원
   ↓ 10초
앱/브라우저 → 온보딩 (4단계)
   ↓ 20초
"🌊 바다 + BALANCE" 즉시 재생
   ↓ ~30초
🍃 첫 자연풍 체험 ← "30초 감동"
   ↓ 매일 사용
홈 → 프리셋/일반 선풍기 → Context 추천
   ↓ 주 1회
Studio → 나만의 작곡 → Playlist 저장
   ↓ 주 1회
Schedule → 자동 실행
   ↓ 한 달 후
Chart 회고 → "내 사용 패턴 확인"
```

---

12. 최종 결론

v0203의 3대 개념이 완성되었습니다:

1. 🎨 Wind Design Studio — "바람을 작곡하다"
2. 🎵 Wind Playlist — "작곡한 바람을 재생하다"
3. 🌀 Normal Fan — "급할 땐 일반 선풍기로"

이 세 가지가 유기적으로 연결되어:

· 첫 사용자: 30초 만에 감동
· 일상 사용: 원터치 + 이퀄라이저 + Snap
· 파워 유저: Studio로 작곡 + Schedule 자동화 + Chart 회고

"선풍기"에서 "감성 기기"로의 진화가 완성되었습니다.

---

Stage 7 완료. v0203 최종 완성.

전체 8주 로드맵 완료 — 다음은 릴리스 준비만 남았습니다.

· Stage 1: 데이터 모델 + CRUD ✓
· Stage 2: Session 엔진 ✓
· Stage 3: REST API + Now Playing Bar ✓
· Stage 4: Studio + Playlist ✓
· Stage 5: Schedule 자동화 ✓
· Stage 6: Visualizer + Context ✓
· Stage 7: Simulation + Chart + 마무리 ✓

"바람을 작곡하고, 재생하고, 어디서든 제어하며, 언제든 회고하는" v0203 제품이 완성됩니다.
